{"parentUuid":"a645faa3-9194-49b9-b5aa-1cd09dda1046","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"f289eda1-327c-4f45-b999-620f23fc1caf","agentId":"ac24982ebd356bd9e","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KD5TYKr9qhmkdjd8q5qRBz","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tdefmodule Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoom do\n2\t  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n3\t  War Room dedicated page (enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page) —\n4\t  `/admin/logs/war-room` (session list + pre-session scoping form) and\n5\t  `/admin/logs/war-room/:room_id` (session detail/drill-down).\n6\t\n7\t  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page.md\n8\t  Story Amendments (2026-07-08) are authoritative wherever they conflict\n9\t  with the original AC text.\n10\t\n11\t  ## ONE module, two router actions (amendment 1)\n12\t\n13\t  Mirrors the sibling `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`'s own \"one module, two\n14\t  concerns\" precedent, but WITHOUT a `handle_params/3` callback: `:show`'s\n15\t  `room_id` is a static PATH segment, available directly in `mount/3`'s own\n16\t  `params` argument (`%{\"room_id\" => id}`) — no `push_patch/2`-driven URL\n17\t  round trip is needed (the exclusive-scope checkboxes and scope-mode\n18\t  switching are ordinary `phx-change`/assigns state, not URL-driven). This\n19\t  means `live_isolated/3` (which never sets `socket.router`, and therefore\n20\t  ALWAYS calls `mount/3` with the literal atom `:not_mounted_at_router`, per\n21\t  `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s own documented precedent) can mount this module\n22\t  for every list-mode test — the `%{\"room_id\" => id}` clause simply never\n23\t  matches that atom, falling through to list mode. Only the DETAIL view and\n24\t  the route-repoint proof need the real router.\n25\t\n26\t  `@active_nav` is `\"logs-war-room\"` (the already-shipped sidebar child key\n27\t  from `admin-sidebar-nesting`) in BOTH actions.\n28\t\n29\t  ## Embedded panel retirement (amendment 2)\n30\t\n31\t  `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoomPanel` no longer renders on `/admin/logs` —\n32\t  the \"start a new session\" form lives here now. `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`\n33\t  keeps its own War Room *status poll* (for the sidebar dot / topbar pill,\n34\t  amendment 4) but no longer starts/stops sessions itself.\n35\t\n36\t  ## AC8's live-activity preview reuses `TraceMapBubble` for level+source\n37\t  only (amendment 3)\n38\t\n39\t  NOT a second radial Leitstand map (that already moved to\n40\t  `/admin/logs/trace-map`) — a lightweight, `:readonly`-mode reuse of the\n41\t  same bubble component already used on `/admin/logs`, previewing the\n42\t  LEVEL and SOURCE dimensions only. The actual \"sync is noisy\" motivation\n43\t  is solved directly by the exclusive-scope checkboxes (AC9–AC11), not by\n44\t  this preview.\n45\t\n46\t  ## Exclusive scope (AC9/AC10, Dev Notes Option A)\n47\t\n48\t  `war_room_sessions.exclusive_scope` is a separate, nullable JSONB column\n49\t  (migration 000021) — `scope` stays a plain string. Choosing `\"exclusive\"`\n50\t  in the scope selector stores `scope: \"all\"` + a non-empty\n51\t  `exclusive_scope` list (the \"capture everything EXCEPT these endpoints\"\n52\t  convenience). The exclude-list checkboxes are sourced from\n53\t  `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap.long_polling_endpoints/0` (amendment 5 —\n54\t  reused, not a hardcoded parallel list) plus a free-text \"other\" input\n55\t  (AC9); the LiveView itself (`merge_exclusive_scope/1`) merges both into\n56\t  one list before calling into `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session/1`,\n57\t  which performs its own defense-in-depth shape validation (Dev Notes\n58\t  item 7) — this LiveView never trusts its own client-side UI as the sole\n59\t  guard.\n60\t\n61\t  ## Sidebar dot / topbar pill (amendment 4)\n62\t\n63\t  This module also threads `:war_room_active`/`:war_room_started_at`/\n64\t  `:war_room_started_by` through to `Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1`, polled the\n65\t  same 2s-interval way `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`/`Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`\n66\t  already poll `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.status/0` for their own war-room\n67\t  banners.\n68\t\n69\t  ## Copy-to-markdown (AC14, amendment 6)\n70\t\n71\t  Reuses the EXISTING, already-CSP-safe `nebu:copy-thread` client event\n72\t  `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.DetailPanel`'s own copy-thread button already\n73\t  dispatches (`priv/static/assets/app.js`'s generic, document-level\n74\t  listener needs no changes for this reuse) — no new inline JS. The\n75\t  \"Copied to clipboard\" toast is a DEDICATED `@copy_toast` assign, not\n76\t  Phoenix's built-in `@flash` (this admin layout does not render `@flash`\n77\t  today — mirrors `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`'s own `@cascade_flash`\n78\t  precedent).\n79\t\n80\t  ## HTML-escaping (security)\n81\t\n82\t  `exclusive_scope`/`exclusive_other` values (and every other\n83\t  admin/operator-supplied field: `reason`, `filter`, custom routes) are\n84\t  ALWAYS rendered through ordinary HEEx `{...}` interpolation — auto-escaped\n85\t  by Phoenix on every render, including inside `data-copy-text` attribute\n86\t  values. `raw/1`/`Phoenix.HTML.raw/1`/`safe_html` helpers are never used on\n87\t  any of these fields anywhere in this module.\n88\t  \"\"\"\n89\t  use Phoenix.LiveView, layout: {Nebu.Admin.Layouts, :live}\n90\t\n91\t  alias Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap\n92\t  alias Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapBubble\n93\t  alias Nebu.Admin.WarRoomIndicator\n94\t  alias Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager\n95\t  alias Phoenix.LiveView.JS\n96\t\n97\t  @topic \"operational_logs\"\n98\t\n99\t  @levels [\"error\", \"warning\", \"info\", \"debug\"]\n100\t  @sources [\"http\", \"ecto\", \"other\"]\n101\t\n102\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n103\t  # Mount\n104\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n105\t\n106\t  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n107\t  def mount(params, session, socket) do\n108\t    Phoenix.PubSub.subscribe(Nebu.PubSub, @topic)\n109\t\n110\t    socket =\n111\t      socket\n112\t      |> assign(:active_nav, \"logs-war-room\")\n113\t      |> assign(:page_title, \"War Room\")\n114\t      |> assign(:current_admin_id, admin_id_from_session(session))\n115\t      |> WarRoomIndicator.assign_status(WarRoomIndicator.status())\n116\t\n117\t    socket =\n118\t      case room_id_param(params) do\n119\t        nil -> mount_list(socket)\n120\t        room_id -> mount_detail(socket, room_id)\n121\t      end\n122\t\n123\t    if connected?(socket) and Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n124\t      WarRoomIndicator.schedule()\n125\t    end\n126\t\n127\t    {:ok, socket}\n128\t  end\n129\t\n130\t  # `params` is the literal atom `:not_mounted_at_router` for every\n131\t  # `live_isolated/3` mount (see moduledoc) — never matches the map pattern\n132\t  # below, falling through to `nil` (list mode).\n133\t  defp room_id_param(%{\"room_id\" => id}) when is_binary(id) and id != \"\", do: id\n134\t  defp room_id_param(_params), do: nil\n135\t\n136\t  defp admin_id_from_session(%{\"current_user\" => %{\"sub\" => sub}}) when is_binary(sub), do: sub\n137\t\n138\t  defp admin_id_from_session(%{\"current_user\" => %{\"matrix_user_id\" => uid}})\n139\t       when is_binary(uid),\n140\t       do: uid\n141\t\n142\t  defp admin_id_from_session(_session), do: \"unknown-admin\"\n143\t\n144\t  defp mount_list(socket) do\n145\t    socket\n146\t    |> assign(:mode, :list)\n147\t    |> assign(:sessions, load_sessions())\n148\t    |> assign(:form_scope, \"all\")\n149\t    |> assign(:start_error, nil)\n150\t    |> assign(:levels, @levels)\n151\t    |> assign(:sources, @sources)\n152\t    |> assign(:level_counts, Map.new(@levels, &{&1, 0}))\n153\t    |> assign(:source_counts, Map.new(@sources, &{&1, 0}))\n154\t    |> seed_preview_counts()\n155\t  end\n156\t\n157\t  defp mount_detail(socket, room_id) do\n158\t    session = load_session(room_id)\n159\t\n160\t    socket\n161\t    |> assign(:mode, :detail)\n162\t    |> assign(:room_id, room_id)\n163\t    |> assign(:session, session)\n164\t    |> assign(:entries, if(session, do: load_entries(room_id), else: []))\n165\t    |> assign(:copy_toast, \"\")\n166\t  end\n167\t\n168\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n169\t  # handle_info — PubSub live-activity preview (AC8) + war-room status poll\n170\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n171\t\n172\t  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n173\t  def handle_info(%{level: _level, source: _source, request_id: _request_id} = entry, socket) do\n174\t    if socket.assigns.mode == :list do\n175\t      {:noreply, ingest_preview(socket, entry)}\n176\t    else\n177\t      {:noreply, socket}\n178\t    end\n179\t  end\n180\t\n181\t  def handle_info(:refresh_war_room_status, socket) do\n182\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo), do: WarRoomIndicator.schedule()\n183\t\n184\t    {:noreply,\n185\t     WarRoomIndicator.assign_status(socket, WarRoomIndicator.status())}\n186\t  end\n187\t\n188\t  def handle_info(_other, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}\n189\t\n190\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n191\t  # handle_event\n192\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n193\t\n194\t  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n195\t  def handle_event(\"scope_changed\", params, socket) do\n196\t    {:noreply, assign(socket, :form_scope, params[\"scope\"])}\n197\t  end\n198\t\n199\t  def handle_event(\"start_war_room\", params, socket) do\n200\t    reason = String.trim(params[\"reason\"] || \"\")\n201\t    scope = blank_to_nil(params[\"scope\"])\n202\t\n203\t    {stored_scope, exclusive_scope} =\n204\t      if scope == \"exclusive\" do\n205\t        {\"all\", merge_exclusive_scope(params)}\n206\t      else\n207\t        {scope, nil}\n208\t      end\n209\t\n210\t    attrs = %{\n211\t      started_by: socket.assigns.current_admin_id,\n212\t      reason: reason,\n213\t      scope: stored_scope,\n214\t      filter: war_room_filter(params[\"user_id\"], params[\"device_id\"]),\n215\t      exclusive_scope: exclusive_scope,\n216\t      max_entries: parse_int(params[\"max_entries\"], 100),\n217\t      max_duration_s: parse_int(params[\"max_duration_s\"], 300)\n218\t    }\n219\t\n220\t    case WarRoomManager.start_session(attrs) do\n221\t      {:ok, _session} ->\n222\t        {:noreply,\n223\t         socket\n224\t         |> assign(:sessions, load_sessions())\n225\t         |> assign(:start_error, nil)\n226\t         |> assign(:form_scope, \"all\")\n227\t         |> WarRoomIndicator.assign_status(WarRoomIndicator.status())}\n228\t\n229\t      {:error, _kind, message} ->\n230\t        {:noreply, assign(socket, :start_error, message)}\n231\t    end\n232\t  end\n233\t\n234\t  # AC2 of war-room-mode: at most one session may be active at a time — while\n235\t  # one is, the list page shows a \"War Room Active\" banner (mirrors the\n236\t  # RETIRED embedded panel's own active-state UI, `war-room-panel`/\n237\t  # `stop-war-room-button` testids, amendment 2 wording \"may be reused... if\n238\t  # convenient\") in place of the start form, with a stop control.\n239\t  def handle_event(\"stop_war_room\", %{\"room_id\" => room_id}, socket) do\n240\t    WarRoomManager.stop_session(room_id)\n241\t\n242\t    {:noreply,\n243\t     socket\n244\t     |> assign(:sessions, load_sessions())\n245\t     |> WarRoomIndicator.assign_status(WarRoomIndicator.status())}\n246\t  end\n247\t\n248\t  def handle_event(\"copy_to_markdown\", _params, socket) do\n249\t    {:noreply, assign(socket, :copy_toast, \"Copied to clipboard\")}\n250\t  end\n251\t\n252\t  # ── AC9/AC10: exclusive_scope merging (checkboxes + free-text \"other\") ────\n253\t\n254\t  defp merge_exclusive_scope(params) do\n255\t    checked =\n256\t      params\n257\t      |> Map.get(\"exclusive_scope\", [])\n258\t      |> List.wrap()\n259\t      |> Enum.filter(&(is_binary(&1) and &1 != \"\"))\n260\t\n261\t    other = params |> Map.get(\"exclusive_other\", \"\") |> to_string() |> String.trim()\n262\t\n263\t    list = if other == \"\", do: checked, else: checked ++ [other]\n264\t\n265\t    case Enum.uniq(list) do\n266\t      [] -> nil\n267\t      merged -> merged\n268\t    end\n269\t  end\n270\t\n271\t  defp war_room_filter(user_id, device_id) do\n272\t    entries =\n273\t      [{\"user_id\", blank_to_nil(user_id)}, {\"device_id\", blank_to_nil(device_id)}]\n274\t      |> Enum.reject(fn {_k, v} -> is_nil(v) end)\n275\t\n276\t    case entries do\n277\t      [] -> nil\n278\t      pairs -> Map.new(pairs)\n279\t    end\n280\t  end\n281\t\n282\t  defp blank_to_nil(nil), do: nil\n283\t  defp blank_to_nil(\"\"), do: nil\n284\t  defp blank_to_nil(value), do: value\n285\t\n286\t  defp parse_int(value, default) do\n287\t    case blank_to_nil(value) do\n288\t      nil ->\n289\t        default\n290\t\n291\t      v ->\n292\t        case Integer.parse(to_string(v)) do\n293\t          {n, _rest} -> n\n294\t          :error -> default\n295\t        end\n296\t    end\n297\t  end\n298\t\n299\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n300\t  # Live-activity preview (AC8, amendment 3) — level + source only\n301\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n302\t\n303\t  defp seed_preview_counts(socket) do\n304\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n305\t      {:ok, rows} = Nebu.Web.LogStore.get_logs(%{})\n306\t      Enum.reduce(Enum.take(rows, 500), socket, &ingest_preview(&2, &1))\n307\t    else\n308\t      socket\n309\t    end\n310\t  end\n311\t\n312\t  defp ingest_preview(socket, entry) do\n313\t    level = normalize_level(entry[:level])\n314\t    source = bucket_source(entry[:source])\n315\t\n316\t    socket\n317\t    |> update(:level_counts, &Map.update(&1, level, 1, fn c -> c + 1 end))\n318\t    |> update(:source_counts, &Map.update(&1, source, 1, fn c -> c + 1 end))\n319\t  end\n320\t\n321\t  defp normalize_level(level) when is_binary(level), do: level\n322\t  defp normalize_level(level) when is_atom(level) and not is_nil(level), do: Atom.to_string(level)\n323\t  defp normalize_level(_), do: \"info\"\n324\t\n325\t  defp bucket_source(\"http\"), do: \"http\"\n326\t  defp bucket_source(\"ecto\"), do: \"ecto\"\n327\t  defp bucket_source(_), do: \"other\"\n328\t\n329\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n330\t  # Data access — session list / detail (this module owns its own inline\n331\t  # SQL, ADR-018-compliant, mirroring Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom/\n332\t  # Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager's own precedent)\n333\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n334\t\n335\t  @session_columns \"id, started_by, reason, scope, filter, exclusive_scope, max_entries, \" <>\n336\t                      \"max_duration_s, started_at, ended_at, ended_reason, entries_collected\"\n337\t\n338\t  # AC1: active sessions first, then most-recent-start-first.\n339\t  defp load_sessions do\n340\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n341\t      result =\n342\t        Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n343\t          Nebu.Repo,\n344\t          \"\"\"\n345\t          SELECT #{@session_columns}\n346\t          FROM war_room_sessions\n347\t          ORDER BY CASE WHEN ended_at IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END, started_at DESC\n348\t          \"\"\",\n349\t          []\n350\t        )\n351\t\n352\t      Enum.map(result.rows, &row_to_session/1)\n353\t    else\n354\t      []\n355\t    end\n356\t  end\n357\t\n358\t  defp load_session(room_id) do\n359\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n360\t      # `room_id` arrives as the canonical hex-dash STRING (a real path\n361\t      # segment) — Postgrex's `uuid` extension requires the raw 16-byte\n362\t      # BINARY form for a bound parameter against a `uuid` column (confirmed\n363\t      # against the live stack: a plain string `$1` raises\n364\t      # `DBConnection.EncodeError`), mirroring\n365\t      # `Nebu.Web.LogStore.get_logs/1`'s own `normalize_filter_value/2`\n366\t      # precedent for the identical `war_room_id` case.\n367\t      with {:ok, binary_id} <- Ecto.UUID.cast(room_id) |> uuid_dump() do\n368\t        result =\n369\t          Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n370\t            Nebu.Repo,\n371\t            \"SELECT #{@session_columns} FROM war_room_sessions WHERE id = $1\",\n372\t            [binary_id]\n373\t          )\n374\t\n375\t        case result.rows do\n376\t          [row] -> row_to_session(row)\n377\t          [] -> nil\n378\t        end\n379\t      else\n380\t        :error -> nil\n381\t      end\n382\t    else\n383\t      nil\n384\t    end\n385\t  rescue\n386\t    # Defensive: a malformed (non-UUID-shaped) room_id in the URL must show\n387\t    # AC6's 404, never crash the page.\n388\t    _ -> nil\n389\t  end\n390\t\n391\t  defp uuid_dump({:ok, canonical}), do: {:ok, Ecto.UUID.dump!(canonical)}\n392\t  defp uuid_dump(:error), do: :error\n393\t\n394\t  defp load_entries(room_id) do\n395\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n396\t      {:ok, rows} = Nebu.Web.LogStore.get_logs(%{war_room_id: room_id})\n397\t      # get_logs/1 returns most-recent-first; AC5 requires ascending\n398\t      # chronological order for the captured-logs section.\n399\t      Enum.reverse(rows)\n400\t    else\n401\t      []\n402\t    end\n403\t  end\n404\t\n405\t  defp row_to_session([\n406\t         id,\n407\t         started_by,\n408\t         reason,\n409\t         scope,\n410\t         filter,\n411\t         exclusive_scope,\n412\t         max_entries,\n413\t         max_duration_s,\n414\t         started_at,\n415\t         ended_at,\n416\t         ended_reason,\n417\t         entries_collected\n418\t       ]) do\n419\t    %{\n420\t      # `id` is a native Postgres `uuid` column — a raw `Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!/3`\n421\t      # (this module's own inline SQL, ADR-018) returns it as a 16-byte\n422\t      # BINARY, not the canonical hex-dash STRING (see\n423\t      # `Nebu.Web.LogStore.get_logs/1`'s own moduledoc comment on this exact\n424\t      # Postgrex behavior) — converted here since `id` is rendered directly\n425\t      # into `data-room-id`/`href`/markdown text throughout this module.\n426\t      id: Ecto.UUID.load!(id),\n427\t      started_by: started_by,\n428\t      reason: reason,\n429\t      scope: scope,\n430\t      filter: filter,\n431\t      exclusive_scope: exclusive_scope,\n432\t      max_entries: max_entries,\n433\t      max_duration_s: max_duration_s,\n434\t      started_at: started_at,\n435\t      ended_at: ended_at,\n436\t      ended_reason: ended_reason,\n437\t      entries_collected: entries_collected\n438\t    }\n439\t  end\n440\t\n441\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n442\t  # View-model helpers\n443\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n444\t\n445\t  # AC1 guarantees an active session (ended_at IS NULL) sorts first when one\n446\t  # exists — AC2 of war-room-mode guarantees at most one such row exists at\n447\t  # all.\n448\t  defp active_session([%{ended_at: nil} = session | _rest]), do: session\n449\t  defp active_session(_sessions), do: nil\n450\t\n451\t  defp scope_label(session) do\n452\t    base = session.scope || \"all\"\n453\t\n454\t    case session.exclusive_scope do\n455\t      nil -> base\n456\t      [] -> base\n457\t      excl -> base <> \" except \" <> Enum.join(excl, \", \")\n458\t    end\n459\t  end\n460\t\n461\t  defp filter_summary(nil), do: \"—\"\n462\t  defp filter_summary(filter) when filter == %{}, do: \"—\"\n463\t\n464\t  defp filter_summary(filter) when is_map(filter) do\n465\t    Enum.map_join(filter, \", \", fn {k, v} -> \"#{k}: #{v}\" end)\n466\t  end\n467\t\n468\t  defp status_badge_text(%{ended_at: nil}), do: \"🟢 Active\"\n469\t\n470\t  defp status_badge_text(%{ended_reason: reason}) do\n471\t    \"🔴 Ended (\" <> String.replace(reason || \"unknown\", \"_\", \" \") <> \")\"\n472\t  end\n473\t\n474\t  defp entries_badge_text(session), do: \"#{session.entries_collected}/#{session.max_entries}\"\n475\t\n476\t  defp time_range_text(%{ended_at: nil, started_at: started_at}) do\n477\t    \"started \" <> ago_text(started_at) <> \" ago\"\n478\t  end\n479\t\n480\t  defp time_range_text(%{started_at: started_at, ended_at: ended_at}) do\n481\t    format_dt(started_at) <> \" → \" <> format_dt(ended_at)\n482\t  end\n483\t\n484\t  defp duration_text(%{ended_at: nil, started_at: started_at, max_duration_s: max_duration_s}) do\n485\t    elapsed = DateTime.diff(DateTime.utc_now(), started_at, :second)\n486\t    remaining = max(max_duration_s - elapsed, 0)\n487\t    format_clock(remaining) <> \" remaining\"\n488\t  end\n489\t\n490\t  defp duration_text(%{started_at: started_at, ended_at: ended_at}) do\n491\t    format_clock(max(DateTime.diff(ended_at, started_at, :second), 0))\n492\t  end\n493\t\n494\t  defp ago_text(started_at) do\n495\t    seconds = max(DateTime.diff(DateTime.utc_now(), started_at, :second), 0)\n496\t\n497\t    cond do\n498\t      seconds < 60 -> \"#{seconds}s\"\n499\t      seconds < 3600 -> \"#{div(seconds, 60)}m\"\n500\t      true -> \"#{div(seconds, 3600)}h\"\n501\t    end\n502\t  end\n503\t\n504\t  defp format_dt(nil), do: \"—\"\n505\t  defp format_dt(%DateTime{} = dt), do: Calendar.strftime(dt, \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\")\n506\t  defp format_dt(dt), do: to_string(dt)\n507\t\n508\t  defp format_clock(total_seconds) when total_seconds >= 3600 do\n509\t    hours = div(total_seconds, 3600)\n510\t    rest = rem(total_seconds, 3600)\n511\t    \"#{pad2(hours)}:#{pad2(div(rest, 60))}:#{pad2(rem(rest, 60))}\"\n512\t  end\n513\t\n514\t  defp format_clock(total_seconds) do\n515\t    \"#{pad2(div(total_seconds, 60))}:#{pad2(rem(total_seconds, 60))}\"\n516\t  end\n517\t\n518\t  defp pad2(n), do: String.pad_leading(Integer.to_string(n), 2, \"0\")\n519\t\n520\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n521\t  # Copy-to-markdown (AC14, Dev Notes template — matched exactly by the ATDD\n522\t  # regexes, see moduledoc)\n523\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n524\t\n525\t  defp markdown_text(session, entries) do\n526\t    \"\"\"\n527\t    # War Room Session #{session.id}\n528\t\n529\t    **Started by:** #{session.started_by}\n530\t    **Reason:** #{session.reason}\n531\t    **Scope:** #{scope_label(session)}\n532\t    **Filter:** #{filter_summary(session.filter)}\n533\t    **Time:** #{format_dt(session.started_at)} → #{if session.ended_at, do: format_dt(session.ended_at), else: \"active\"}\n534\t    **Entries collected:** #{session.entries_collected} / #{session.max_entries}\n535\t\n536\t    ## Captured Logs\n537\t\n538\t    #{Enum.map_join(entries, \"\\n\", &markdown_entry_line/1)}\n539\t    \"\"\"\n540\t  end\n541\t\n542\t  defp markdown_entry_line(entry) do\n543\t    \"- `#{entry.inserted_at}` [#{entry.level}] [#{entry.source}] #{entry.message}\"\n544\t  end\n545\t\n546\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n547\t  # Render\n548\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n549\t\n550\t  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n551\t  def render(assigns) do\n552\t    ~H\"\"\"\n553\t    <div class=\"flex items-center justify-between mb-4\">\n554\t      <h1 class=\"text-heading font-semibold\">War Room</h1>\n555\t      <.link\n556\t        :if={@mode == :detail}\n557\t        navigate=\"/admin/logs/war-room\"\n558\t        data-testid=\"war-room-detail-back-link\"\n559\t        class=\"link link-primary text-sm\"\n560\t      >\n561\t        ← Back to War Room sessions\n562\t      </.link>\n563\t    </div>\n564\t\n565\t    <.render_list :if={@mode == :list} {assigns} />\n566\t    <.render_detail :if={@mode == :detail} {assigns} />\n567\t    \"\"\"\n568\t  end\n569\t\n570\t  defp render_list(assigns) do\n571\t    assigns = assign(assigns, :active_session, active_session(assigns.sessions))\n572\t\n573\t    ~H\"\"\"\n574\t    <section aria-label=\"Start a new War Room session\" class=\"mb-6\">\n575\t      <h2 class=\"font-semibold mb-2\">Start a new War Room session</h2>\n576\t\n577\t      <div data-testid=\"war-room-trace-preview\" class=\"mb-3\">\n578\t        <h3 class=\"text-sm font-medium text-text-secondary mb-1\">Live activity (level + source)</h3>\n579\t        <div class=\"flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-2\">\n580\t          <TraceMapBubble.trace_map_bubble\n581\t            :for={lvl <- @levels}\n582\t            dimension={:level}\n583\t            value={lvl}\n584\t            count={Map.get(@level_counts, lvl, 0)}\n585\t            active={false}\n586\t            mode={:readonly}\n587\t            on_click={nil}\n588\t          />\n589\t        </div>\n590\t        <div class=\"flex flex-wrap gap-2\">\n591\t          <TraceMapBubble.trace_map_bubble\n592\t            :for={src <- @sources}\n593\t            dimension={:source}\n594\t            value={src}\n595\t            count={Map.get(@source_counts, src, 0)}\n596\t            active={false}\n597\t            mode={:readonly}\n598\t            on_click={nil}\n599\t          />\n600\t        </div>\n601\t      </div>\n602\t\n603\t      <%!-- AC2 of war-room-mode: at most one active session at a time — while\n604\t            one is active, this banner (reusing the RETIRED embedded panel's\n605\t            own `war-room-panel`/`stop-war-room-button` testids, amendment 2:\n606\t            \"may be reused... if convenient\") replaces the start form. --%>\n607\t      <div\n608\t        :if={@active_session}\n609\t        data-testid=\"war-room-panel\"\n610\t        aria-label=\"War Room\"\n611\t        class=\"mb-4 rounded-lg border-2 border-error bg-error/5 p-4 max-w-md\"\n612\t      >\n613\t        <div class=\"flex items-center justify-between mb-2 gap-2\">\n614\t          <h3 class=\"font-semibold text-error\">🚨 War Room Active</h3>\n615\t          <button\n616\t            type=\"button\"\n617\t            data-testid=\"stop-war-room-button\"\n618\t            phx-click=\"stop_war_room\"\n619\t            phx-value-room_id={@active_session.id}\n620\t            class=\"btn btn-sm btn-error\"\n621\t          >\n622\t            Stop War Room\n623\t          </button>\n624\t        </div>\n625\t        <p data-testid=\"war-room-status\" class=\"text-sm\">\n626\t          {entries_badge_text(@active_session)} entries · started by {@active_session.started_by}\n627\t          for reason: {@active_session.reason}\n628\t        </p>\n629\t      </div>\n630\t\n631\t      <form\n632\t        :if={is_nil(@active_session)}\n633\t        id=\"war-room-start-form\"\n634\t        data-testid=\"war-room-start-form\"\n635\t        phx-submit=\"start_war_room\"\n636\t        class=\"flex flex-col gap-2 max-w-md\"\n637\t      >\n638\t        <label class=\"form-control\">\n639\t          <span class=\"label-text\">Reason</span>\n640\t          <input\n641\t            type=\"text\"\n642\t            name=\"reason\"\n643\t            required\n644\t            placeholder=\"e.g. sync bug investigation\"\n645\t            class=\"input input-bordered input-sm\"\n646\t          />\n647\t        </label>\n648\t\n649\t        <label class=\"form-control\">\n650\t          <span class=\"label-text\">Scope</span>\n651\t          <select\n652\t            name=\"scope\"\n653\t            data-testid=\"war-room-scope-select\"\n654\t            phx-change=\"scope_changed\"\n655\t            class=\"select select-bordered select-sm\"\n656\t          >\n657\t            <option value=\"\">-- select scope --</option>\n658\t            <option value=\"all\" selected={@form_scope == \"all\"}>all endpoints</option>\n659\t            <option value=\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\">/_matrix/client/v3/sync</option>\n660\t            <option value=\"exclusive\">exclusive (all except…)</option>\n661\t          </select>\n662\t        </label>\n663\t\n664\t        <div\n665\t          :if={@form_scope == \"exclusive\"}\n666\t          data-testid=\"war-room-exclusive-options\"\n667\t          class=\"border border-base-300 rounded p-2 flex flex-col gap-1\"\n668\t        >\n669\t          <p class=\"text-xs text-text-secondary mb-1\">Exclude these endpoints from capture:</p>\n670\t          <label :for={route <- TraceMap.long_polling_endpoints()} class=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-sm\">\n671\t            <input type=\"checkbox\" name=\"exclusive_scope[]\" value={route} />\n672\t            <span class=\"font-mono\">{route}</span>\n673\t          </label>\n674\t          <label class=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-sm mt-1\">\n675\t            <span>other:</span>\n676\t            <input\n677\t              type=\"text\"\n678\t              name=\"exclusive_other\"\n679\t              placeholder=\"/custom/noisy/route\"\n680\t              class=\"input input-bordered input-xs\"\n681\t            />\n682\t          </label>\n683\t        </div>\n684\t\n685\t        <label class=\"form-control\">\n686\t          <span class=\"label-text\">user_id (optional)</span>\n687\t          <input type=\"text\" name=\"user_id\" placeholder=\"@alice:localhost\" class=\"input input-bordered input-sm\" />\n688\t        </label>\n689\t\n690\t        <label class=\"form-control\">\n691\t          <span class=\"label-text\">device_id (optional)</span>\n692\t          <input type=\"text\" name=\"device_id\" placeholder=\"GHTYAJSQZD\" class=\"input input-bordered input-sm\" />\n693\t        </label>\n694\t\n695\t        <label class=\"form-control\">\n696\t          <span class=\"label-text\">max_entries</span>\n697\t          <input\n698\t            type=\"number\"\n699\t            name=\"max_entries\"\n700\t            value=\"100\"\n701\t            min=\"10\"\n702\t            max=\"10000\"\n703\t            class=\"input input-bordered input-sm\"\n704\t          />\n705\t        </label>\n706\t\n707\t        <label class=\"form-control\">\n708\t          <span class=\"label-text\">max_duration_s</span>\n709\t          <input\n710\t            type=\"number\"\n711\t            name=\"max_duration_s\"\n712\t            value=\"300\"\n713\t            min=\"10\"\n714\t            max=\"3600\"\n715\t            class=\"input input-bordered input-sm\"\n716\t          />\n717\t        </label>\n718\t\n719\t        <div\n720\t          data-testid=\"war-room-warning-all\"\n721\t          class=\"alert alert-warning text-sm\"\n722\t          style=\"display:none;\"\n723\t          role=\"status\"\n724\t        >\n725\t          Warning: collecting all endpoints. High-frequency endpoints like `/sync` can generate 1000s of\n726\t          entries per second. Recommend narrowing scope (by user_id/device_id) or keeping\n727\t          max_entries/max_duration_s conservative.\n728\t        </div>\n729\t\n730\t        <div\n731\t          data-testid=\"war-room-warning-sync\"\n732\t          class=\"alert alert-error text-sm\"\n733\t          style=\"display:none;\"\n734\t          role=\"alert\"\n735\t        >\n736\t          ⚠️ `/sync` is called continuously by every client. Unless you are targeting a single\n737\t          user/device, this will fill your log budget in seconds. Require max_entries ≤ 100 and\n738\t          max_duration_s ≤ 300, or cancel.\n739\t        </div>\n740\t\n741\t        <p :if={@start_error} data-testid=\"war-room-start-error\" class=\"text-sm text-status-error-text\" role=\"alert\">\n742\t          {@start_error}\n743\t        </p>\n744\t\n745\t        <button type=\"submit\" data-testid=\"start-war-room-button\" class=\"btn btn-error btn-sm\">\n746\t          🚨 Start War Room\n747\t        </button>\n748\t      </form>\n749\t    </section>\n750\t\n751\t    <section aria-label=\"War Room sessions\">\n752\t      <h2 class=\"font-semibold mb-2\">Sessions</h2>\n753\t\n754\t      <div :if={@sessions == []} data-testid=\"war-room-empty-state\" class=\"text-sm text-text-secondary\">\n755\t        No War Room sessions yet. Start one from the monitoring page (<code>/admin/logs</code>) to begin\n756\t        diagnostic logging.\n757\t      </div>\n758\t\n759\t      <div :if={@sessions != []} class=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n760\t        <.link\n761\t          :for={session <- @sessions}\n762\t          navigate={\"/admin/logs/war-room/\" <> session.id}\n763\t          data-testid=\"war-room-session-row\"\n764\t          data-room-id={session.id}\n765\t          class=\"block card card-compact bg-base-100 border border-base-300 p-3 hover:bg-base-200 focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary\"\n766\t        >\n767\t          <div class=\"flex items-center gap-3 text-sm flex-wrap\">\n768\t            <span data-testid=\"war-room-session-status\" class=\"font-semibold\">{status_badge_text(session)}</span>\n769\t            <span data-testid=\"war-room-session-started-by\" class=\"font-mono text-xs\">{session.started_by}</span>\n770\t            <span data-testid=\"war-room-session-reason\">{session.reason}</span>\n771\t            <span data-testid=\"war-room-session-scope\" class=\"font-mono text-xs\">{scope_label(session)}</span>\n772\t            <span data-testid=\"war-room-session-filter\" class=\"text-xs text-text-secondary\">\n773\t              {filter_summary(session.filter)}\n774\t            </span>\n775\t            <span data-testid=\"war-room-session-time-range\" class=\"text-xs text-text-secondary\">\n776\t              {time_range_text(session)}\n777\t            </span>\n778\t            <span data-testid=\"war-room-session-entries\" class=\"badge badge-sm\">{entries_badge_text(session)}</span>\n779\t            <span data-testid=\"war-room-session-duration\" class=\"font-mono text-xs\">{duration_text(session)}</span>\n780\t          </div>\n781\t        </.link>\n782\t      </div>\n783\t    </section>\n784\t    \"\"\"\n785\t  end\n786\t\n787\t  defp render_detail(assigns) do\n788\t    ~H\"\"\"\n789\t    <%= if @session do %>\n790\t      <section data-testid=\"war-room-detail-meta\" aria-label=\"Session details\" class=\"mb-4 rounded-lg border border-base-300 p-4\">\n791\t        <h2 class=\"font-semibold mb-2\">Session {@session.id}</h2>\n792\t        <p><span data-testid=\"war-room-session-status\">{status_badge_text(@session)}</span></p>\n793\t        <p>Started by: {@session.started_by}</p>\n794\t        <p>Reason: {@session.reason}</p>\n795\t        <p>Scope: {scope_label(@session)}</p>\n796\t        <p>Filter: {filter_summary(@session.filter)}</p>\n797\t        <p>Budget: {@session.max_entries} entries / {@session.max_duration_s}s</p>\n798\t        <p>Started at: {format_dt(@session.started_at)}</p>\n799\t        <p>Ended at: {if @session.ended_at, do: format_dt(@session.ended_at), else: \"—\"}</p>\n800\t        <p>Ended reason: {@session.ended_reason || \"—\"}</p>\n801\t        <p>Entries collected: {entries_badge_text(@session)}</p>\n802\t\n803\t        <button\n804\t          type=\"button\"\n805\t          data-testid=\"war-room-copy-markdown-button\"\n806\t          data-copy-text={markdown_text(@session, @entries)}\n807\t          phx-click={JS.dispatch(\"nebu:copy-thread\") |> JS.push(\"copy_to_markdown\")}\n808\t          aria-label=\"Copy session summary to clipboard as markdown\"\n809\t          class=\"btn btn-sm btn-ghost mt-2\"\n810\t        >\n811\t          Copy to Markdown\n812\t        </button>\n813\t      </section>\n814\t\n815\t      <p\n816\t        :if={@copy_toast != \"\"}\n817\t        data-testid=\"war-room-copy-toast\"\n818\t        role=\"status\"\n819\t        class=\"alert alert-success text-sm mb-3\"\n820\t      >\n821\t        {@copy_toast}\n822\t      </p>\n823\t\n824\t      <h2 class=\"font-semibold mb-2\">Logs captured by this session</h2>\n825\t      <div aria-label=\"Captured request stream\" class=\"flex flex-col gap-1\">\n826\t        <div\n827\t          :for={entry <- @entries}\n828\t          data-testid=\"request-row\"\n829\t          data-request-id={entry.request_id}\n830\t          class=\"card card-compact bg-base-100 border-l-4 border-base-300 p-3\"\n831\t        >\n832\t          <div class=\"flex items-center gap-3 text-sm flex-wrap\">\n833\t            <span class=\"font-mono text-xs text-text-secondary whitespace-nowrap\">{format_dt(entry.inserted_at)}</span>\n834\t            <span class=\"badge badge-sm\">{entry.level}</span>\n835\t            <span class=\"text-xs text-text-secondary\">{entry.source}</span>\n836\t            <span class=\"font-mono text-xs\">{entry.route_template}</span>\n837\t            <span>{entry.message}</span>\n838\t          </div>\n839\t        </div>\n840\t      </div>\n841\t    <% else %>\n842\t      <div data-testid=\"war-room-not-found\" class=\"alert alert-error\">\n843\t        War Room session not found.\n844\t      </div>\n845\t    <% end %>\n846\t    \"\"\"\n847\t  end\n848\tend\n849\t"}]},"uuid":"72172d6b-1445-4443-bcc1-19ba4a3e4ba4","timestamp":"2026-07-08T13:58:28.804Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/war_room.ex","content":"defmodule Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoom do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  War Room dedicated page (enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page) —\n  `/admin/logs/war-room` (session list + pre-session scoping form) and\n  `/admin/logs/war-room/:room_id` (session detail/drill-down).\n\n  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page.md\n  Story Amendments (2026-07-08) are authoritative wherever they conflict\n  with the original AC text.\n\n  ## ONE module, two router actions (amendment 1)\n\n  Mirrors the sibling `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`'s own \"one module, two\n  concerns\" precedent, but WITHOUT a `handle_params/3` callback: `:show`'s\n  `room_id` is a static PATH segment, available directly in `mount/3`'s own\n  `params` argument (`%{\"room_id\" => id}`) — no `push_patch/2`-driven URL\n  round trip is needed (the exclusive-scope checkboxes and scope-mode\n  switching are ordinary `phx-change`/assigns state, not URL-driven). This\n  means `live_isolated/3` (which never sets `socket.router`, and therefore\n  ALWAYS calls `mount/3` with the literal atom `:not_mounted_at_router`, per\n  `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s own documented precedent) can mount this module\n  for every list-mode test — the `%{\"room_id\" => id}` clause simply never\n  matches that atom, falling through to list mode. Only the DETAIL view and\n  the route-repoint proof need the real router.\n\n  `@active_nav` is `\"logs-war-room\"` (the already-shipped sidebar child key\n  from `admin-sidebar-nesting`) in BOTH actions.\n\n  ## Embedded panel retirement (amendment 2)\n\n  `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoomPanel` no longer renders on `/admin/logs` —\n  the \"start a new session\" form lives here now. `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`\n  keeps its own War Room *status poll* (for the sidebar dot / topbar pill,\n  amendment 4) but no longer starts/stops sessions itself.\n\n  ## AC8's live-activity preview reuses `TraceMapBubble` for level+source\n  only (amendment 3)\n\n  NOT a second radial Leitstand map (that already moved to\n  `/admin/logs/trace-map`) — a lightweight, `:readonly`-mode reuse of the\n  same bubble component already used on `/admin/logs`, previewing the\n  LEVEL and SOURCE dimensions only. The actual \"sync is noisy\" motivation\n  is solved directly by the exclusive-scope checkboxes (AC9–AC11), not by\n  this preview.\n\n  ## Exclusive scope (AC9/AC10, Dev Notes Option A)\n\n  `war_room_sessions.exclusive_scope` is a separate, nullable JSONB column\n  (migration 000021) — `scope` stays a plain string. Choosing `\"exclusive\"`\n  in the scope selector stores `scope: \"all\"` + a non-empty\n  `exclusive_scope` list (the \"capture everything EXCEPT these endpoints\"\n  convenience). The exclude-list checkboxes are sourced from\n  `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap.long_polling_endpoints/0` (amendment 5 —\n  reused, not a hardcoded parallel list) plus a free-text \"other\" input\n  (AC9); the LiveView itself (`merge_exclusive_scope/1`) merges both into\n  one list before calling into `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session/1`,\n  which performs its own defense-in-depth shape validation (Dev Notes\n  item 7) — this LiveView never trusts its own client-side UI as the sole\n  guard.\n\n  ## Sidebar dot / topbar pill (amendment 4)\n\n  This module also threads `:war_room_active`/`:war_room_started_at`/\n  `:war_room_started_by` through to `Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1`, polled the\n  same 2s-interval way `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`/`Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`\n  already poll `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.status/0` for their own war-room\n  banners.\n\n  ## Copy-to-markdown (AC14, amendment 6)\n\n  Reuses the EXISTING, already-CSP-safe `nebu:copy-thread` client event\n  `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.DetailPanel`'s own copy-thread button already\n  dispatches (`priv/static/assets/app.js`'s generic, document-level\n  listener needs no changes for this reuse) — no new inline JS. The\n  \"Copied to clipboard\" toast is a DEDICATED `@copy_toast` assign, not\n  Phoenix's built-in `@flash` (this admin layout does not render `@flash`\n  today — mirrors `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`'s own `@cascade_flash`\n  precedent).\n\n  ## HTML-escaping (security)\n\n  `exclusive_scope`/`exclusive_other` values (and every other\n  admin/operator-supplied field: `reason`, `filter`, custom routes) are\n  ALWAYS rendered through ordinary HEEx `{...}` interpolation — auto-escaped\n  by Phoenix on every render, including inside `data-copy-text` attribute\n  values. `raw/1`/`Phoenix.HTML.raw/1`/`safe_html` helpers are never used on\n  any of these fields anywhere in this module.\n  \"\"\"\n  use Phoenix.LiveView, layout: {Nebu.Admin.Layouts, :live}\n\n  alias Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap\n  alias Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapBubble\n  alias Nebu.Admin.WarRoomIndicator\n  alias Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager\n  alias Phoenix.LiveView.JS\n\n  @topic \"operational_logs\"\n\n  @levels [\"error\", \"warning\", \"info\", \"debug\"]\n  @sources [\"http\", \"ecto\", \"other\"]\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Mount\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n  def mount(params, session, socket) do\n    Phoenix.PubSub.subscribe(Nebu.PubSub, @topic)\n\n    socket =\n      socket\n      |> assign(:active_nav, \"logs-war-room\")\n      |> assign(:page_title, \"War Room\")\n      |> assign(:current_admin_id, admin_id_from_session(session))\n      |> WarRoomIndicator.assign_status(WarRoomIndicator.status())\n\n    socket =\n      case room_id_param(params) do\n        nil -> mount_list(socket)\n        room_id -> mount_detail(socket, room_id)\n      end\n\n    if connected?(socket) and Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n      WarRoomIndicator.schedule()\n    end\n\n    {:ok, socket}\n  end\n\n  # `params` is the literal atom `:not_mounted_at_router` for every\n  # `live_isolated/3` mount (see moduledoc) — never matches the map pattern\n  # below, falling through to `nil` (list mode).\n  defp room_id_param(%{\"room_id\" => id}) when is_binary(id) and id != \"\", do: id\n  defp room_id_param(_params), do: nil\n\n  defp admin_id_from_session(%{\"current_user\" => %{\"sub\" => sub}}) when is_binary(sub), do: sub\n\n  defp admin_id_from_session(%{\"current_user\" => %{\"matrix_user_id\" => uid}})\n       when is_binary(uid),\n       do: uid\n\n  defp admin_id_from_session(_session), do: \"unknown-admin\"\n\n  defp mount_list(socket) do\n    socket\n    |> assign(:mode, :list)\n    |> assign(:sessions, load_sessions())\n    |> assign(:form_scope, \"all\")\n    |> assign(:start_error, nil)\n    |> assign(:levels, @levels)\n    |> assign(:sources, @sources)\n    |> assign(:level_counts, Map.new(@levels, &{&1, 0}))\n    |> assign(:source_counts, Map.new(@sources, &{&1, 0}))\n    |> seed_preview_counts()\n  end\n\n  defp mount_detail(socket, room_id) do\n    session = load_session(room_id)\n\n    socket\n    |> assign(:mode, :detail)\n    |> assign(:room_id, room_id)\n    |> assign(:session, session)\n    |> assign(:entries, if(session, do: load_entries(room_id), else: []))\n    |> assign(:copy_toast, \"\")\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # handle_info — PubSub live-activity preview (AC8) + war-room status poll\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n  def handle_info(%{level: _level, source: _source, request_id: _request_id} = entry, socket) do\n    if socket.assigns.mode == :list do\n      {:noreply, ingest_preview(socket, entry)}\n    else\n      {:noreply, socket}\n    end\n  end\n\n  def handle_info(:refresh_war_room_status, socket) do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo), do: WarRoomIndicator.schedule()\n\n    {:noreply,\n     WarRoomIndicator.assign_status(socket, WarRoomIndicator.status())}\n  end\n\n  def handle_info(_other, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # handle_event\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n  def handle_event(\"scope_changed\", params, socket) do\n    {:noreply, assign(socket, :form_scope, params[\"scope\"])}\n  end\n\n  def handle_event(\"start_war_room\", params, socket) do\n    reason = String.trim(params[\"reason\"] || \"\")\n    scope = blank_to_nil(params[\"scope\"])\n\n    {stored_scope, exclusive_scope} =\n      if scope == \"exclusive\" do\n        {\"all\", merge_exclusive_scope(params)}\n      else\n        {scope, nil}\n      end\n\n    attrs = %{\n      started_by: socket.assigns.current_admin_id,\n      reason: reason,\n      scope: stored_scope,\n      filter: war_room_filter(params[\"user_id\"], params[\"device_id\"]),\n      exclusive_scope: exclusive_scope,\n      max_entries: parse_int(params[\"max_entries\"], 100),\n      max_duration_s: parse_int(params[\"max_duration_s\"], 300)\n    }\n\n    case WarRoomManager.start_session(attrs) do\n      {:ok, _session} ->\n        {:noreply,\n         socket\n         |> assign(:sessions, load_sessions())\n         |> assign(:start_error, nil)\n         |> assign(:form_scope, \"all\")\n         |> WarRoomIndicator.assign_status(WarRoomIndicator.status())}\n\n      {:error, _kind, message} ->\n        {:noreply, assign(socket, :start_error, message)}\n    end\n  end\n\n  # AC2 of war-room-mode: at most one session may be active at a time — while\n  # one is, the list page shows a \"War Room Active\" banner (mirrors the\n  # RETIRED embedded panel's own active-state UI, `war-room-panel`/\n  # `stop-war-room-button` testids, amendment 2 wording \"may be reused... if\n  # convenient\") in place of the start form, with a stop control.\n  def handle_event(\"stop_war_room\", %{\"room_id\" => room_id}, socket) do\n    WarRoomManager.stop_session(room_id)\n\n    {:noreply,\n     socket\n     |> assign(:sessions, load_sessions())\n     |> WarRoomIndicator.assign_status(WarRoomIndicator.status())}\n  end\n\n  def handle_event(\"copy_to_markdown\", _params, socket) do\n    {:noreply, assign(socket, :copy_toast, \"Copied to clipboard\")}\n  end\n\n  # ── AC9/AC10: exclusive_scope merging (checkboxes + free-text \"other\") ────\n\n  defp merge_exclusive_scope(params) do\n    checked =\n      params\n      |> Map.get(\"exclusive_scope\", [])\n      |> List.wrap()\n      |> Enum.filter(&(is_binary(&1) and &1 != \"\"))\n\n    other = params |> Map.get(\"exclusive_other\", \"\") |> to_string() |> String.trim()\n\n    list = if other == \"\", do: checked, else: checked ++ [other]\n\n    case Enum.uniq(list) do\n      [] -> nil\n      merged -> merged\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp war_room_filter(user_id, device_id) do\n    entries =\n      [{\"user_id\", blank_to_nil(user_id)}, {\"device_id\", blank_to_nil(device_id)}]\n      |> Enum.reject(fn {_k, v} -> is_nil(v) end)\n\n    case entries do\n      [] -> nil\n      pairs -> Map.new(pairs)\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp blank_to_nil(nil), do: nil\n  defp blank_to_nil(\"\"), do: nil\n  defp blank_to_nil(value), do: value\n\n  defp parse_int(value, default) do\n    case blank_to_nil(value) do\n      nil ->\n        default\n\n      v ->\n        case Integer.parse(to_string(v)) do\n          {n, _rest} -> n\n          :error -> default\n        end\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Live-activity preview (AC8, amendment 3) — level + source only\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  defp seed_preview_counts(socket) do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n      {:ok, rows} = Nebu.Web.LogStore.get_logs(%{})\n      Enum.reduce(Enum.take(rows, 500), socket, &ingest_preview(&2, &1))\n    else\n      socket\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp ingest_preview(socket, entry) do\n    level = normalize_level(entry[:level])\n    source = bucket_source(entry[:source])\n\n    socket\n    |> update(:level_counts, &Map.update(&1, level, 1, fn c -> c + 1 end))\n    |> update(:source_counts, &Map.update(&1, source, 1, fn c -> c + 1 end))\n  end\n\n  defp normalize_level(level) when is_binary(level), do: level\n  defp normalize_level(level) when is_atom(level) and not is_nil(level), do: Atom.to_string(level)\n  defp normalize_level(_), do: \"info\"\n\n  defp bucket_source(\"http\"), do: \"http\"\n  defp bucket_source(\"ecto\"), do: \"ecto\"\n  defp bucket_source(_), do: \"other\"\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Data access — session list / detail (this module owns its own inline\n  # SQL, ADR-018-compliant, mirroring Nebu.Web.LogStore.WarRoom/\n  # Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager's own precedent)\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  @session_columns \"id, started_by, reason, scope, filter, exclusive_scope, max_entries, \" <>\n                      \"max_duration_s, started_at, ended_at, ended_reason, entries_collected\"\n\n  # AC1: active sessions first, then most-recent-start-first.\n  defp load_sessions do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n      result =\n        Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n          Nebu.Repo,\n          \"\"\"\n          SELECT #{@session_columns}\n          FROM war_room_sessions\n          ORDER BY CASE WHEN ended_at IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END, started_at DESC\n          \"\"\",\n          []\n        )\n\n      Enum.map(result.rows, &row_to_session/1)\n    else\n      []\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp load_session(room_id) do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n      # `room_id` arrives as the canonical hex-dash STRING (a real path\n      # segment) — Postgrex's `uuid` extension requires the raw 16-byte\n      # BINARY form for a bound parameter against a `uuid` column (confirmed\n      # against the live stack: a plain string `$1` raises\n      # `DBConnection.EncodeError`), mirroring\n      # `Nebu.Web.LogStore.get_logs/1`'s own `normalize_filter_value/2`\n      # precedent for the identical `war_room_id` case.\n      with {:ok, binary_id} <- Ecto.UUID.cast(room_id) |> uuid_dump() do\n        result =\n          Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!(\n            Nebu.Repo,\n            \"SELECT #{@session_columns} FROM war_room_sessions WHERE id = $1\",\n            [binary_id]\n          )\n\n        case result.rows do\n          [row] -> row_to_session(row)\n          [] -> nil\n        end\n      else\n        :error -> nil\n      end\n    else\n      nil\n    end\n  rescue\n    # Defensive: a malformed (non-UUID-shaped) room_id in the URL must show\n    # AC6's 404, never crash the page.\n    _ -> nil\n  end\n\n  defp uuid_dump({:ok, canonical}), do: {:ok, Ecto.UUID.dump!(canonical)}\n  defp uuid_dump(:error), do: :error\n\n  defp load_entries(room_id) do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n      {:ok, rows} = Nebu.Web.LogStore.get_logs(%{war_room_id: room_id})\n      # get_logs/1 returns most-recent-first; AC5 requires ascending\n      # chronological order for the captured-logs section.\n      Enum.reverse(rows)\n    else\n      []\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp row_to_session([\n         id,\n         started_by,\n         reason,\n         scope,\n         filter,\n         exclusive_scope,\n         max_entries,\n         max_duration_s,\n         started_at,\n         ended_at,\n         ended_reason,\n         entries_collected\n       ]) do\n    %{\n      # `id` is a native Postgres `uuid` column — a raw `Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query!/3`\n      # (this module's own inline SQL, ADR-018) returns it as a 16-byte\n      # BINARY, not the canonical hex-dash STRING (see\n      # `Nebu.Web.LogStore.get_logs/1`'s own moduledoc comment on this exact\n      # Postgrex behavior) — converted here since `id` is rendered directly\n      # into `data-room-id`/`href`/markdown text throughout this module.\n      id: Ecto.UUID.load!(id),\n      started_by: started_by,\n      reason: reason,\n      scope: scope,\n      filter: filter,\n      exclusive_scope: exclusive_scope,\n      max_entries: max_entries,\n      max_duration_s: max_duration_s,\n      started_at: started_at,\n      ended_at: ended_at,\n      ended_reason: ended_reason,\n      entries_collected: entries_collected\n    }\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # View-model helpers\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  # AC1 guarantees an active session (ended_at IS NULL) sorts first when one\n  # exists — AC2 of war-room-mode guarantees at most one such row exists at\n  # all.\n  defp active_session([%{ended_at: nil} = session | _rest]), do: session\n  defp active_session(_sessions), do: nil\n\n  defp scope_label(session) do\n    base = session.scope || \"all\"\n\n    case session.exclusive_scope do\n      nil -> base\n      [] -> base\n      excl -> base <> \" except \" <> Enum.join(excl, \", \")\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp filter_summary(nil), do: \"—\"\n  defp filter_summary(filter) when filter == %{}, do: \"—\"\n\n  defp filter_summary(filter) when is_map(filter) do\n    Enum.map_join(filter, \", \", fn {k, v} -> \"#{k}: #{v}\" end)\n  end\n\n  defp status_badge_text(%{ended_at: nil}), do: \"🟢 Active\"\n\n  defp status_badge_text(%{ended_reason: reason}) do\n    \"🔴 Ended (\" <> String.replace(reason || \"unknown\", \"_\", \" \") <> \")\"\n  end\n\n  defp entries_badge_text(session), do: \"#{session.entries_collected}/#{session.max_entries}\"\n\n  defp time_range_text(%{ended_at: nil, started_at: started_at}) do\n    \"started \" <> ago_text(started_at) <> \" ago\"\n  end\n\n  defp time_range_text(%{started_at: started_at, ended_at: ended_at}) do\n    format_dt(started_at) <> \" → \" <> format_dt(ended_at)\n  end\n\n  defp duration_text(%{ended_at: nil, started_at: started_at, max_duration_s: max_duration_s}) do\n    elapsed = DateTime.diff(DateTime.utc_now(), started_at, :second)\n    remaining = max(max_duration_s - elapsed, 0)\n    format_clock(remaining) <> \" remaining\"\n  end\n\n  defp duration_text(%{started_at: started_at, ended_at: ended_at}) do\n    format_clock(max(DateTime.diff(ended_at, started_at, :second), 0))\n  end\n\n  defp ago_text(started_at) do\n    seconds = max(DateTime.diff(DateTime.utc_now(), started_at, :second), 0)\n\n    cond do\n      seconds < 60 -> \"#{seconds}s\"\n      seconds < 3600 -> \"#{div(seconds, 60)}m\"\n      true -> \"#{div(seconds, 3600)}h\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  defp format_dt(nil), do: \"—\"\n  defp format_dt(%DateTime{} = dt), do: Calendar.strftime(dt, \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\")\n  defp format_dt(dt), do: to_string(dt)\n\n  defp format_clock(total_seconds) when total_seconds >= 3600 do\n    hours = div(total_seconds, 3600)\n    rest = rem(total_seconds, 3600)\n    \"#{pad2(hours)}:#{pad2(div(rest, 60))}:#{pad2(rem(rest, 60))}\"\n  end\n\n  defp format_clock(total_seconds) do\n    \"#{pad2(div(total_seconds, 60))}:#{pad2(rem(total_seconds, 60))}\"\n  end\n\n  defp pad2(n), do: String.pad_leading(Integer.to_string(n), 2, \"0\")\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Copy-to-markdown (AC14, Dev Notes template — matched exactly by the ATDD\n  # regexes, see moduledoc)\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  defp markdown_text(session, entries) do\n    \"\"\"\n    # War Room Session #{session.id}\n\n    **Started by:** #{session.started_by}\n    **Reason:** #{session.reason}\n    **Scope:** #{scope_label(session)}\n    **Filter:** #{filter_summary(session.filter)}\n    **Time:** #{format_dt(session.started_at)} → #{if session.ended_at, do: format_dt(session.ended_at), else: \"active\"}\n    **Entries collected:** #{session.entries_collected} / #{session.max_entries}\n\n    ## Captured Logs\n\n    #{Enum.map_join(entries, \"\\n\", &markdown_entry_line/1)}\n    \"\"\"\n  end\n\n  defp markdown_entry_line(entry) do\n    \"- `#{entry.inserted_at}` [#{entry.level}] [#{entry.source}] #{entry.message}\"\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Render\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n  def render(assigns) do\n    ~H\"\"\"\n    <div class=\"flex items-center justify-between mb-4\">\n      <h1 class=\"text-heading font-semibold\">War Room</h1>\n      <.link\n        :if={@mode == :detail}\n        navigate=\"/admin/logs/war-room\"\n        data-testid=\"war-room-detail-back-link\"\n        class=\"link link-primary text-sm\"\n      >\n        ← Back to War Room sessions\n      </.link>\n    </div>\n\n    <.render_list :if={@mode == :list} {assigns} />\n    <.render_detail :if={@mode == :detail} {assigns} />\n    \"\"\"\n  end\n\n  defp render_list(assigns) do\n    assigns = assign(assigns, :active_session, active_session(assigns.sessions))\n\n    ~H\"\"\"\n    <section aria-label=\"Start a new War Room session\" class=\"mb-6\">\n      <h2 class=\"font-semibold mb-2\">Start a new War Room session</h2>\n\n      <div data-testid=\"war-room-trace-preview\" class=\"mb-3\">\n        <h3 class=\"text-sm font-medium text-text-secondary mb-1\">Live activity (level + source)</h3>\n        <div class=\"flex flex-wrap gap-2 mb-2\">\n          <TraceMapBubble.trace_map_bubble\n            :for={lvl <- @levels}\n            dimension={:level}\n            value={lvl}\n            count={Map.get(@level_counts, lvl, 0)}\n            active={false}\n            mode={:readonly}\n            on_click={nil}\n          />\n        </div>\n        <div class=\"flex flex-wrap gap-2\">\n          <TraceMapBubble.trace_map_bubble\n            :for={src <- @sources}\n            dimension={:source}\n            value={src}\n            count={Map.get(@source_counts, src, 0)}\n            active={false}\n            mode={:readonly}\n            on_click={nil}\n          />\n        </div>\n      </div>\n\n      <%!-- AC2 of war-room-mode: at most one active session at a time — while\n            one is active, this banner (reusing the RETIRED embedded panel's\n            own `war-room-panel`/`stop-war-room-button` testids, amendment 2:\n            \"may be reused... if convenient\") replaces the start form. --%>\n      <div\n        :if={@active_session}\n        data-testid=\"war-room-panel\"\n        aria-label=\"War Room\"\n        class=\"mb-4 rounded-lg border-2 border-error bg-error/5 p-4 max-w-md\"\n      >\n        <div class=\"flex items-center justify-between mb-2 gap-2\">\n          <h3 class=\"font-semibold text-error\">🚨 War Room Active</h3>\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            data-testid=\"stop-war-room-button\"\n            phx-click=\"stop_war_room\"\n            phx-value-room_id={@active_session.id}\n            class=\"btn btn-sm btn-error\"\n          >\n            Stop War Room\n          </button>\n        </div>\n        <p data-testid=\"war-room-status\" class=\"text-sm\">\n          {entries_badge_text(@active_session)} entries · started by {@active_session.started_by}\n          for reason: {@active_session.reason}\n        </p>\n      </div>\n\n      <form\n        :if={is_nil(@active_session)}\n        id=\"war-room-start-form\"\n        data-testid=\"war-room-start-form\"\n        phx-submit=\"start_war_room\"\n        class=\"flex flex-col gap-2 max-w-md\"\n      >\n        <label class=\"form-control\">\n          <span class=\"label-text\">Reason</span>\n          <input\n            type=\"text\"\n            name=\"reason\"\n            required\n            placeholder=\"e.g. sync bug investigation\"\n            class=\"input input-bordered input-sm\"\n          />\n        </label>\n\n        <label class=\"form-control\">\n          <span class=\"label-text\">Scope</span>\n          <select\n            name=\"scope\"\n            data-testid=\"war-room-scope-select\"\n            phx-change=\"scope_changed\"\n            class=\"select select-bordered select-sm\"\n          >\n            <option value=\"\">-- select scope --</option>\n            <option value=\"all\" selected={@form_scope == \"all\"}>all endpoints</option>\n            <option value=\"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\">/_matrix/client/v3/sync</option>\n            <option value=\"exclusive\">exclusive (all except…)</option>\n          </select>\n        </label>\n\n        <div\n          :if={@form_scope == \"exclusive\"}\n          data-testid=\"war-room-exclusive-options\"\n          class=\"border border-base-300 rounded p-2 flex flex-col gap-1\"\n        >\n          <p class=\"text-xs text-text-secondary mb-1\">Exclude these endpoints from capture:</p>\n          <label :for={route <- TraceMap.long_polling_endpoints()} class=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-sm\">\n            <input type=\"checkbox\" name=\"exclusive_scope[]\" value={route} />\n            <span class=\"font-mono\">{route}</span>\n          </label>\n          <label class=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-sm mt-1\">\n            <span>other:</span>\n            <input\n              type=\"text\"\n              name=\"exclusive_other\"\n              placeholder=\"/custom/noisy/route\"\n              class=\"input input-bordered input-xs\"\n            />\n          </label>\n        </div>\n\n        <label class=\"form-control\">\n          <span class=\"label-text\">user_id (optional)</span>\n          <input type=\"text\" name=\"user_id\" placeholder=\"@alice:localhost\" class=\"input input-bordered input-sm\" />\n        </label>\n\n        <label class=\"form-control\">\n          <span class=\"label-text\">device_id (optional)</span>\n          <input type=\"text\" name=\"device_id\" placeholder=\"GHTYAJSQZD\" class=\"input input-bordered input-sm\" />\n        </label>\n\n        <label class=\"form-control\">\n          <span class=\"label-text\">max_entries</span>\n          <input\n            type=\"number\"\n            name=\"max_entries\"\n            value=\"100\"\n            min=\"10\"\n            max=\"10000\"\n            class=\"input input-bordered input-sm\"\n          />\n        </label>\n\n        <label class=\"form-control\">\n          <span class=\"label-text\">max_duration_s</span>\n          <input\n            type=\"number\"\n            name=\"max_duration_s\"\n            value=\"300\"\n            min=\"10\"\n            max=\"3600\"\n            class=\"input input-bordered input-sm\"\n          />\n        </label>\n\n        <div\n          data-testid=\"war-room-warning-all\"\n          class=\"alert alert-warning text-sm\"\n          style=\"display:none;\"\n          role=\"status\"\n        >\n          Warning: collecting all endpoints. High-frequency endpoints like `/sync` can generate 1000s of\n          entries per second. Recommend narrowing scope (by user_id/device_id) or keeping\n          max_entries/max_duration_s conservative.\n        </div>\n\n        <div\n          data-testid=\"war-room-warning-sync\"\n          class=\"alert alert-error text-sm\"\n          style=\"display:none;\"\n          role=\"alert\"\n        >\n          ⚠️ `/sync` is called continuously by every client. Unless you are targeting a single\n          user/device, this will fill your log budget in seconds. Require max_entries ≤ 100 and\n          max_duration_s ≤ 300, or cancel.\n        </div>\n\n        <p :if={@start_error} data-testid=\"war-room-start-error\" class=\"text-sm text-status-error-text\" role=\"alert\">\n          {@start_error}\n        </p>\n\n        <button type=\"submit\" data-testid=\"start-war-room-button\" class=\"btn btn-error btn-sm\">\n          🚨 Start War Room\n        </button>\n      </form>\n    </section>\n\n    <section aria-label=\"War Room sessions\">\n      <h2 class=\"font-semibold mb-2\">Sessions</h2>\n\n      <div :if={@sessions == []} data-testid=\"war-room-empty-state\" class=\"text-sm text-text-secondary\">\n        No War Room sessions yet. Start one from the monitoring page (<code>/admin/logs</code>) to begin\n        diagnostic logging.\n      </div>\n\n      <div :if={@sessions != []} class=\"flex flex-col gap-2\">\n        <.link\n          :for={session <- @sessions}\n          navigate={\"/admin/logs/war-room/\" <> session.id}\n          data-testid=\"war-room-session-row\"\n          data-room-id={session.id}\n          class=\"block card card-compact bg-base-100 border border-base-300 p-3 hover:bg-base-200 focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-primary\"\n        >\n          <div class=\"flex items-center gap-3 text-sm flex-wrap\">\n            <span data-testid=\"war-room-session-status\" class=\"font-semibold\">{status_badge_text(session)}</span>\n            <span data-testid=\"war-room-session-started-by\" class=\"font-mono text-xs\">{session.started_by}</span>\n            <span data-testid=\"war-room-session-reason\">{session.reason}</span>\n            <span data-testid=\"war-room-session-scope\" class=\"font-mono text-xs\">{scope_label(session)}</span>\n            <span data-testid=\"war-room-session-filter\" class=\"text-xs text-text-secondary\">\n              {filter_summary(session.filter)}\n            </span>\n            <span data-testid=\"war-room-session-time-range\" class=\"text-xs text-text-secondary\">\n              {time_range_text(session)}\n            </span>\n            <span data-testid=\"war-room-session-entries\" class=\"badge badge-sm\">{entries_badge_text(session)}</span>\n            <span data-testid=\"war-room-session-duration\" class=\"font-mono text-xs\">{duration_text(session)}</span>\n          </div>\n        </.link>\n      </div>\n    </section>\n    \"\"\"\n  end\n\n  defp render_detail(assigns) do\n    ~H\"\"\"\n    <%= if @session do %>\n      <section data-testid=\"war-room-detail-meta\" aria-label=\"Session details\" class=\"mb-4 rounded-lg border border-base-300 p-4\">\n        <h2 class=\"font-semibold mb-2\">Session {@session.id}</h2>\n        <p><span data-testid=\"war-room-session-status\">{status_badge_text(@session)}</span></p>\n        <p>Started by: {@session.started_by}</p>\n        <p>Reason: {@session.reason}</p>\n        <p>Scope: {scope_label(@session)}</p>\n        <p>Filter: {filter_summary(@session.filter)}</p>\n        <p>Budget: {@session.max_entries} entries / {@session.max_duration_s}s</p>\n        <p>Started at: {format_dt(@session.started_at)}</p>\n        <p>Ended at: {if @session.ended_at, do: format_dt(@session.ended_at), else: \"—\"}</p>\n        <p>Ended reason: {@session.ended_reason || \"—\"}</p>\n        <p>Entries collected: {entries_badge_text(@session)}</p>\n\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          data-testid=\"war-room-copy-markdown-button\"\n          data-copy-text={markdown_text(@session, @entries)}\n          phx-click={JS.dispatch(\"nebu:copy-thread\") |> JS.push(\"copy_to_markdown\")}\n          aria-label=\"Copy session summary to clipboard as markdown\"\n          class=\"btn btn-sm btn-ghost mt-2\"\n        >\n          Copy to Markdown\n        </button>\n      </section>\n\n      <p\n        :if={@copy_toast != \"\"}\n        data-testid=\"war-room-copy-toast\"\n        role=\"status\"\n        class=\"alert alert-success text-sm mb-3\"\n      >\n        {@copy_toast}\n      </p>\n\n      <h2 class=\"font-semibold mb-2\">Logs captured by this session</h2>\n      <div aria-label=\"Captured request stream\" class=\"flex flex-col gap-1\">\n        <div\n          :for={entry <- @entries}\n          data-testid=\"request-row\"\n          data-request-id={entry.request_id}\n          class=\"card card-compact bg-base-100 border-l-4 border-base-300 p-3\"\n        >\n          <div class=\"flex items-center gap-3 text-sm flex-wrap\">\n            <span class=\"font-mono text-xs text-text-secondary whitespace-nowrap\">{format_dt(entry.inserted_at)}</span>\n            <span class=\"badge badge-sm\">{entry.level}</span>\n            <span class=\"text-xs text-text-secondary\">{entry.source}</span>\n            <span class=\"font-mono text-xs\">{entry.route_template}</span>\n            <span>{entry.message}</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    <% else %>\n      <div data-testid=\"war-room-not-found\" class=\"alert alert-error\">\n        War Room session not found.\n      </div>\n    <% end %>\n    \"\"\"\n  end\nend\n","numLines":849,"startLine":1,"totalLines":849}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a645faa3-9194-49b9-b5aa-1cd09dda1046","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"cb5bfb2a-e3b6-443f-8a26-cf24d839af5a","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"f289eda1-327c-4f45-b999-620f23fc1caf","agentId":"ac24982ebd356bd9e","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Vs4kFNXRDWpLZChHgPTjvv","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t# Memory\n2\t\n3\t## PRD Accessibility Requirements\n4\t_Filled during First Breath — the specific requirements from the PRD. Hard constraints for every session._\n5\t\n6\t## Component Library\n7\tTailwind + DaisyUI. `btn`/`btn-sm`/`btn-active`/`btn-outline` for toggleable filter bubbles (see `TraceMapBubble`), `badge`/`badge-outline` for chips, `card`/`bg-base-200` for form panels.\n8\t\n9\t## Template Conventions\n10\t**Correction (2026-07-06): this sanctum was inherited from a different (Go-based) project.** The stack described in CREED.md's Dominion section (\"Go Templates\", `gateway/internal/admin/`) is WRONG for this repo. Actual stack: **Phoenix LiveView + HEEx**, admin UI lives at `server/lib/nebu/admin/`. Static/controller pages use `*_html.ex` + `templates/**/*.html.heex`; live pages (`LogsLive`, `DashboardLive`, `BootstrapLive`, `AuditLogLive`) are `Phoenix.LiveView` modules with inline `~H` templates. Shared chrome is `Nebu.Admin.Layouts` (`:live` layout = topbar + left sidebar, `@active_nav` highlights the current item). Sidebar nav is currently a FLAT list (`layouts.ex` `nav_item`s) — no nested/collapsible tree exists yet, even for logically-nested items like `/admin/config/role-mapping`.\n11\tClient JS lives directly in `server/priv/static/assets/app.js` (committed as source, not bundled from a separate `assets/js/` — only CSS goes through a build step via `assets/package.json`'s `tailwindcss` script). CSP is `script-src 'self'`, no `unsafe-inline` — LiveView `phx-click` bound to `Phoenix.LiveView.JS.dispatch/2` custom events, picked up by named `wire*` functions in `app.js` (e.g. `wireTraceMapFilters`), is the established pattern for client-only interactions that must not round-trip to the server.\n12\t\n13\t## Playwright Patterns\n14\t_Base URL, auth helper, selector conventions, test file organization._\n15\t\n16\t## Known Accessibility Gaps\n17\t_Pages or components with known accessibility issues to watch._\n18\t\n19\t## Known Correctness Gaps (non-a11y)\n20\t- **CONFIRMED BUG (2026-07-06, live-reproduced):** `/admin/logs` Trace Map filter bubbles lose their `aria-pressed`/`btn-active` state (and the actual row filtering) within ~10-15s of live traffic, even with zero War Room session active. Root cause: `TraceMapBubble.trace_map_bubble` calls in `logs_live.ex` never pass an `active` attr, so the component's `@active` is a permanent `false` — filter \"pressed\" state lives ONLY in client-DOM (`aria-pressed`, `btn-active` class), set by `app.js`'s `wireTraceMapFilters`. Every new log entry re-renders the bubble list (count badges change), and the server-rendered patch for that comprehension item overwrites the button's attributes back to the `active:false` baseline, wiping the click. Confirmed via Playwright: clicked a bubble (`aria-pressed` → `true`), waited 12s under live traffic, `aria-pressed` was back to `\"false\"` and all 107 rows visible (filter silently cleared). Fix direction: stop layering client-only toggle state on a DOM node whose siblings churn every render — either move filter state into real LiveView assigns driven by URL query params (`push_patch` + `handle_params/3`), or restructure so the live count badge is a sibling node outside the toggle button so the button itself never gets re-patched.\n21\t"}]},"uuid":"379a2395-20ba-47df-8a71-856a96200bc0","timestamp":"2026-07-08T13:58:37.106Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/_bmad/memory/nebu-agent-ux/MEMORY.md","content":"# Memory\n\n## PRD Accessibility Requirements\n_Filled during First Breath — the specific requirements from the PRD. Hard constraints for every session._\n\n## Component Library\nTailwind + DaisyUI. `btn`/`btn-sm`/`btn-active`/`btn-outline` for toggleable filter bubbles (see `TraceMapBubble`), `badge`/`badge-outline` for chips, `card`/`bg-base-200` for form panels.\n\n## Template Conventions\n**Correction (2026-07-06): this sanctum was inherited from a different (Go-based) project.** The stack described in CREED.md's Dominion section (\"Go Templates\", `gateway/internal/admin/`) is WRONG for this repo. Actual stack: **Phoenix LiveView + HEEx**, admin UI lives at `server/lib/nebu/admin/`. Static/controller pages use `*_html.ex` + `templates/**/*.html.heex`; live pages (`LogsLive`, `DashboardLive`, `BootstrapLive`, `AuditLogLive`) are `Phoenix.LiveView` modules with inline `~H` templates. Shared chrome is `Nebu.Admin.Layouts` (`:live` layout = topbar + left sidebar, `@active_nav` highlights the current item). Sidebar nav is currently a FLAT list (`layouts.ex` `nav_item`s) — no nested/collapsible tree exists yet, even for logically-nested items like `/admin/config/role-mapping`.\nClient JS lives directly in `server/priv/static/assets/app.js` (committed as source, not bundled from a separate `assets/js/` — only CSS goes through a build step via `assets/package.json`'s `tailwindcss` script). CSP is `script-src 'self'`, no `unsafe-inline` — LiveView `phx-click` bound to `Phoenix.LiveView.JS.dispatch/2` custom events, picked up by named `wire*` functions in `app.js` (e.g. `wireTraceMapFilters`), is the established pattern for client-only interactions that must not round-trip to the server.\n\n## Playwright Patterns\n_Base URL, auth helper, selector conventions, test file organization._\n\n## Known Accessibility Gaps\n_Pages or components with known accessibility issues to watch._\n\n## Known Correctness Gaps (non-a11y)\n- **CONFIRMED BUG (2026-07-06, live-reproduced):** `/admin/logs` Trace Map filter bubbles lose their `aria-pressed`/`btn-active` state (and the actual row filtering) within ~10-15s of live traffic, even with zero War Room session active. Root cause: `TraceMapBubble.trace_map_bubble` calls in `logs_live.ex` never pass an `active` attr, so the component's `@active` is a permanent `false` — filter \"pressed\" state lives ONLY in client-DOM (`aria-pressed`, `btn-active` class), set by `app.js`'s `wireTraceMapFilters`. Every new log entry re-renders the bubble list (count badges change), and the server-rendered patch for that comprehension item overwrites the button's attributes back to the `active:false` baseline, wiping the click. Confirmed via Playwright: clicked a bubble (`aria-pressed` → `true`), waited 12s under live traffic, `aria-pressed` was back to `\"false\"` and all 107 rows visible (filter silently cleared). Fix direction: stop layering client-only toggle state on a DOM node whose siblings churn every render — either move filter state into real LiveView assigns driven by URL query params (`push_patch` + `handle_params/3`), or restructure so the live count badge is a sibling node outside the toggle button so the button itself never gets re-patched.\n","numLines":21,"startLine":1,"totalLines":21}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"cb5bfb2a-e3b6-443f-8a26-cf24d839af5a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}
