WCAG violations are identified with exact location, requirement, and fix. Advisory findings are noted. The audit runs against the running stack where possible.\n12\t\n13\t## Preparation\n14\t\n15\tRead MEMORY.md for PRD accessibility requirements. These are the hard constraints — not general WCAG best practices, but the specific requirements this project committed to.\n16\t\n17\t## Audit Method\n18\t\n19\t### Static Analysis (always)\n20\t\n21\tReview the template diff for:\n22\t- Missing `alt` attributes on `<img>`\n23\t- Interactive `<div>` or `<span>` without `role` and `tabindex`\n24\t- Form inputs without associated labels\n25\t- Missing ARIA attributes on dynamic content\n26\t- Hardcoded color values that may fail contrast\n27\t\n28\t### Browser-Level (when Playwright MCP available)\n29\t\n30\tUse `playwright` MCP to inspect the rendered page:\n31\t- Tab through all interactive elements — verify keyboard reachability and focus order\n32\t- Run axe-core or similar via `page.evaluate()` for automated checks\n33\t- Verify `aria-live` regions update correctly for dynamic content\n34\t- Check focus management after modal open/close or form submit\n35\t\n36\t```typescript\n37\t// Run axe accessibility check\n38\tconst results = await page.evaluate(async () => {\n39\t  // @ts-ignore\n40\t  return await window.axe.run();\n41\t});\n42\tconst violations = results.violations;\n43\t```\n44\t\n45\t## Output Format\n46\t\n47\t```markdown\n48\t## Accessibility Audit — [story/page]\n49\t\n50\t**PRD requirements checked:** [list]\n51\t\n52\t### Violations (blocking)\n53\t\n54\t| # | Requirement | Element | Location | Fix |\n55\t|---|-------------|---------|----------|-----|\n56\t| 1 | WCAG 1.1.1 Alt text | `<img>` | template line 23 | Add `alt=\"[description]\"` |\n57\t\n58\t### Advisory\n59\t\n60\t| # | Issue | Location | Recommendation |\n61\t|---|-------|----------|----------------|\n62\t\n63\t### Summary\n64\t\n65\tViolations: [N] — block merge\n66\tAdvisory: [N]\n67\t\n68\t**Verdict:** PASS / BLOCKED\n69\t```\n70\t\n71\t## Memory Integration\n72\t\n73\tAfter audit: note any new accessibility pattern or fix that should be added to the component guidelines in MEMORY.md.\n74\t"}]},"uuid":"0c94269f-95ce-4074-990e-6f9f6dbc2448","timestamp":"2026-07-08T13:58:37.806Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/_bmad/memory/nebu-agent-ux/references/accessibility-audit.md","content":"---\nname: accessibility-audit\ncode: accessibility-audit\ndescription: Audit Admin UI pages against PRD accessibility requirements. WCAG violations are blocking findings. Called by nebu-pipeline on ui:true stories.\n---\n\n# Accessibility Audit\n\n## What Success Looks Like\n\nEvery page changed by the current story is checked against PRD accessibility requirements. WCAG violations are identified with exact location, requirement, and fix. Advisory findings are noted. The audit runs against the running stack where possible.\n\n## Preparation\n\nRead MEMORY.md for PRD accessibility requirements. These are the hard constraints — not general WCAG best practices, but the specific requirements this project committed to.\n\n## Audit Method\n\n### Static Analysis (always)\n\nReview the template diff for:\n- Missing `alt` attributes on `<img>`\n- Interactive `<div>` or `<span>` without `role` and `tabindex`\n- Form inputs without associated labels\n- Missing ARIA attributes on dynamic content\n- Hardcoded color values that may fail contrast\n\n### Browser-Level (when Playwright MCP available)\n\nUse `playwright` MCP to inspect the rendered page:\n- Tab through all interactive elements — verify keyboard reachability and focus order\n- Run axe-core or similar via `page.evaluate()` for automated checks\n- Verify `aria-live` regions update correctly for dynamic content\n- Check focus management after modal open/close or form submit\n\n```typescript\n// Run axe accessibility check\nconst results = await page.evaluate(async () => {\n  // @ts-ignore\n  return await window.axe.run();\n});\nconst violations = results.violations;\n```\n\n## Output Format\n\n```markdown\n## Accessibility Audit — [story/page]\n\n**PRD requirements checked:** [list]\n\n### Violations (blocking)\n\n| # | Requirement | Element | Location | Fix |\n|---|-------------|---------|----------|-----|\n| 1 | WCAG 1.1.1 Alt text | `<img>` | template line 23 | Add `alt=\"[description]\"` |\n\n### Advisory\n\n| # | Issue | Location | Recommendation |\n|---|-------|----------|----------------|\n\n### Summary\n\nViolations: [N] — block merge\nAdvisory: [N]\n\n**Verdict:** PASS / BLOCKED\n```\n\n## Memory Integration\n\nAfter audit: note any new accessibility pattern or fix that should be added to the component guidelines in MEMORY.md.\n","numLines":74,"startLine":1,"totalLines":74}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4eb43acf-3ad6-426b-bd43-0dbb275830dc","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

Nebu.Admin.WarRoomIndicator do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  Shared helper for the war-room recording dot/pill assigns\n  (`:war_room_active`/`:war_room_started_at`/`:war_room_started_by`)\n  threaded through `Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1` (enhanced-logging/\n  war-room-dedicated-page, amendment 4).\n\n  Every remaining admin LiveView (`Nebu.Admin.DashboardLive`,\n  `Nebu.Admin.AuditLogLive`, `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`,\n  `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`, `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoom` — the only\n  LiveViews left after admin-http-refactor's controller conversions, i.e.\n  the full \"anywhere in the admin UI\" surface) calls `status/0` at mount and\n  on its own 2s `schedule/0` tick, then `assign_status/2` to set these three\n  assigns. Polling is UNCONDITIONAL once connected (not gated on \"already\n  active\") — the dot/pill must be able to APPEAR later, while the operator\n  is already on an unrelated page, not merely persist while already\n  showing (mirrors `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`'s own pre-existing\n  \"always rescheduled... because the banner must also APPEAR when a session\n  starts later\" war-room poll, generalized here to every remaining\n  LiveView).\n  \"\"\"\n  import Phoenix.Component, only: [assign: 3]\n\n  @refresh_interval_ms 2_000\n  @refresh_message :refresh_war_room_status\n\n  @doc \"The message name `handle_info/2` should match on for the scheduled refresh tick.\"\n  @spec refresh_message() :: atom()\n  def refresh_message, do: @refresh_message\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  A live DB read of the current War Room status — `:none` when\n  `Nebu.Repo` is not running (plain unit-test runs), mirroring every other\n  DB-guarded helper in this codebase.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec status() :: {:ok, map()} | :none\n  def status do\n    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n      Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.status()\n    else\n      :none\n    end\n  end\n\n  @doc \"Schedules the next refresh tick (`schedule/0`'s own `refresh_message/0`, 2s).\"\n  @spec schedule() :: reference()\n  def schedule, do: Process.send_after(self(), @refresh_message, @refresh_interval_ms)\n\n  @doc \"Assigns `:war_room_active`/`:war_room_started_at`/`:war_room_started_by` from `status`.\"\n  @spec assign_status(Phoenix.LiveView.Socket.t(), {:ok, map()} | :none) ::\n          Phoenix.LiveView.Socket.t()\n  def assign_status(socket, {:ok, session}) do\n    socket\n    |> assign(:war_room_active, true)\n    |> assign(:war_room_started_at, session.started_at)\n    |> assign(:war_room_started_by, session.started_by)\n  end\n\n  def assign_status(socket, :none) do\n    socket\n    |> assign(:war_room_active, false)\n    |> assign(:war_room_started_at, nil)\n    |> assign(:war_room_started_by, nil)\n  end\nend\n","numLines":66,"startLine":1,"totalLines":66}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"48b13981-db5e-4ff0-afa2-6495d0e9eb98","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

This module\n  +  still POLLS `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.status/0` every 2s (unchanged\n  +  cadence) so the sidebar recording dot / topbar pill\n  +  (`Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1`, amendment 4) stay live while an operator is\n  +  on this page — see `@war_room_active`/`@war_room_started_at`/\n  +  `@war_room_started_by`. The pre-existing `war-room-viewing-banner`\n  +  (`?war_room_id=...`, a DIFFERENT, read-only feature from `war-room-mode`'s\n  +  own AC12) is unaffected.\n     \"\"\"\n     use Phoenix.LiveView, layout: {Nebu.Admin.Layouts, :live}\n   \n     alias Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.DetailPanel\n     alias Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapBubble\n  -  alias Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoomPanel\n  +  alias Nebu.Admin.WarRoomIndicator\n     alias Phoenix.LiveView.JS\n   \n     @topic \"operational_logs\"\n  @@ -98,23 +112,6 @@ defmodule Nebu.Admin.LogsLive do\n  -  # enhanced-logging/war-room-mode (AC11): default/bounds mirror the story's\n  -  # own AC11 form spec (max_entries default 100, range 10-10000;\n  -  # max_duration_s default 300, range 10-3600). AC8's stronger /sync-specific\n  -  # caps (<=100 / <=300) are advisory-only (see WarRoomPanel's moduledoc) —\n  -  # not enforced here.\n  -  @war_room_max_entries_bounds {10, 10_000}\n  -  @war_room_max_duration_s_bounds {10, 3_600}\n  -  @war_room_default_max_entries 100\n  -  @war_room_default_max_duration_s 300\n  -\n  -  # Re-polls Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.status/0 while a session is active so\n  -  # the live status bar's budget/remaining-time display actually moves\n  -  # without a page reload (AC11's \"live status bar\") — cheap (a single\n  -  # indexed-by-primary-key-ish SELECT of at most one row) and only scheduled\n  -  # while a session is genuinely active.\n  -  @war_room_refresh_interval_ms 2_000\n  -\n     # M3 fix (bug-fix cycle 5, code-review MAJOR finding): caps the number of\n     # DISTINCT `request_id` rows kept in `@entries_by_request`/`@row_order`/\n     # `@row_meta`/the `:rows` stream for the lifetime of this LiveView process.\n  @@ -154,6 +151,7 @@ defmodule Nebu.Admin.LogsLive do\n  +    war_room_status = initial_war_room_status()\n   \n       socket =\n         socket\n  @@ -185,7 +183,10 @@ defmodule Nebu.Admin.LogsLive do\n  - CSP: no inline JS, a plain interval reading a\n  +  // data attribute.\n  +  var WarRoomPill = {\n  +    mounted: function () {\n  +      this.tick();\n  +      this.timer = setInterval(this.tick.bind(this), 1000);\n  +    },\n  +    updated: function () {\n  +      this.tick();\n  +    },\n  +    destroyed: function () {\n  +      if (this.timer) clearInterval(this.timer);\n  +    },\n  +    tick: function () {\n  +      var clock = this.el.querySelector('[data-testid=\"topbar-war-room-pill-clock\"]');\n  +      if (!clock) return;\n  +      var startedAt = parseInt(this.el.getAttribute(\"data-started-at\"), 10);\n  +      if (!startedAt) return;\n  +\n  +      var total = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) - startedAt);\n  +      var minutes = Math.floor(total / 60);\n  +      var seconds = total % 60;\n  +      var pad = function (n) {\n  +        return String(n).padStart(2, \"0\");\n  +      };\n  +      clock.textContent = pad(minutes) + \":\" + pad(seconds);\n  +    },\n  +  };\n  +\n     // enhanced-logging/admin-logs-retention-settings (AC7/AC8/AC11): the\n     // /admin/logs/config page is a plain (dead) controller render — no\n     // LiveView socket exists there — so the reused-for-visual-styling bubble\n  @@ -1546,6 +1582,7 @@\n  +        WarRoomPill: WarRoomPill,\n         },\n       });\n   \n  +37 -0","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"7ef8a2d8-195f-4f80-8102-8cafdd0153d1","timestamp":"2026-07-08T13:58:56.840Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"server/priv/static/assets/app.js | 37 +++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++++\n 1 file changed, 37 insertions(+)\n\n--- Changes ---\n\nserver/priv/static/assets/app.js\n  @@ -903,6 +903,42 @@\n  +  // enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page (amendment 4): ticks the\n  +  // topbar recording pill's mm:ss elapsed clock every second, purely\n  +  // client-side from its own `data-started-at` (unix seconds) — mirrors\n  +  // `traceMapTickWarRoomClock`'s identical mechanism for the Trace Map\n  +  // page's own war-room banner, but wired as its OWN small hook (rather\n  +  // than depending on the TraceMapLeitstand hook, which only mounts on the\n  +  // /admin/logs/trace-map page) since `Nebu.Admin.Layouts` renders the pill\n  +  // on every admin page. content behind it as\n  background). `priv/static/assets/app.js`'s `wireDetailPanelFocus` (a\n  `MutationObserver` on `document.body`) moves focus into this panel's first\n  focusable control (the copy-thread button) when it appears in the DOM, and\n  returns focus to the request row that triggered it when it is removed.\n  NOTE: a full Tab/Shift+Tab focus TRAP is NOT implemented here (only\n {"parentUuid":"21ececdc-b22a-4a85-970b-c159d3f2ba9a","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"f289eda1-327c-4f45-b999-620f23fc1caf","agentId":"ac24982ebd356bd9e","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VCE7SYPLBxRvMQ2QWevUCo","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tdefmodule Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapBubble do\n2\t  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n3\t  Reusable Trace Map \"bubble\" component (enhanced-logging/admin-logs-monitoring-view,\n4\t  AC1/AC2/AC5/AC13/AC14/AC16).\n5\t\n6\t  A single toggleable pill-shaped button that is BOTH legend (shows a live count)\n7\t  and interactive filter. Renders identically regardless of `mode` — the component\n8\t  itself has NO opinion on what clicking it does server-side; it simply wires\n9\t  whatever `on_click` value the caller passes onto the rendered `<button>`'s\n10\t  `phx-click` attribute. That is the entire \"designed for reuse\" contract (see\n11\t  `test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_bubble_test.exs`, Acceptance Test 11):\n12\t\n13\t    * `mode: :filter` (THIS story, `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`) — the caller passes a\n14\t      `Phoenix.LiveView.JS` command list as `on_click` (client-side DOM toggle +\n15\t      a `nebu:trace-bubble-click` custom-event dispatch picked up by\n16\t      `priv/static/assets/app.js` — see that file's `wireTraceMapFilters`).\n17\t      AC3 requires this to be genuinely client-side-only (no `handle_event`\n18\t      server round-trip per click), so `on_click` here is NEVER a bare event-name\n19\t      string in real `LogsLive` usage.\n20\t    * `mode: :configure` (sibling `admin-logs-retention-settings` story, REUSING\n21\t      this same component) — that story passes a bare event-name string as\n22\t      `on_click` (a normal `phx-click=\"configure_retention\"` push), since\n23\t      AC3's client-side-only requirement is scoped to `:filter` mode only.\n24\t    * `mode: :readonly` (enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page, AC8 +\n25\t      amendment 3 — REUSING this same component for the War Room scoping\n26\t      form's live-activity preview, level+source dimensions only) — the\n27\t      bubble is a live-count LEGEND, never a filter: it renders as a\n28\t      genuinely non-interactive, `disabled` `<button>` with NO `phx-click`\n29\t      attribute at all, regardless of what `on_click` value the caller\n30\t      passes (the component itself refuses to wire it up in this mode,\n31\t      rather than merely trusting the caller to pass `on_click: nil`). The\n32\t      accessible name never uses the `:filter` mode's \"Filter by ...\"\n33\t      wording (that phrasing is reserved for a genuinely interactive\n34\t      bubble).\n35\t\n36\t  ## Accessibility (AC13/AC14)\n37\t\n38\t  Every bubble is a native `<button>` (natural Tab order, Space/Enter activation),\n39\t  carries `aria-pressed` (true/false) and a descriptive `aria-label` — never a\n40\t  `<div onclick>`. Level indication pairs an icon with text (never color alone).\n41\t\n42\t  ## \"Off\" (uncollected) bubbles (AC2)\n43\t\n44\t  When `off: true`, the bubble renders a distinct \"⚫ off\" visual marker\n45\t  (`data-bubble-off=\"true\"`) instead of a live count — this represents a\n46\t  STRUCTURAL absence (the level/source is not currently being collected per the\n47\t  retention policy), not \"zero events in the active window\".\n48\t  \"\"\"\n49\t  use Phoenix.Component\n50\t\n51\t  attr(:dimension, :atom, required: true, doc: \"One of :endpoint, :level, :source\")\n52\t\n53\t  attr(:value, :string,\n54\t    required: true,\n55\t    doc: \"The specific value, e.g. \\\"/login\\\", \\\"error\\\", \\\"ecto\\\"\"\n56\t  )\n57\t\n58\t  attr(:count, :integer, required: true, doc: \"Live count of matching events in the current view\")\n59\t  attr(:active, :boolean, default: false, doc: \"Whether this filter is currently active\")\n60\t  attr(:mode, :atom, default: :filter, values: [:filter, :configure, :readonly])\n61\t\n62\t  attr(:on_click, :any,\n63\t    required: true,\n64\t    doc:\n65\t      \"phx-click value: a Phoenix.LiveView.JS command list (:filter) or a bare event name (:configure)\"\n66\t  )\n67\t\n68\t  attr(:off, :boolean,\n69\t    default: false,\n70\t    doc: \"True when this dimension value is not currently collected (AC2)\"\n71\t  )\n72\t\n73\t  attr(:id, :string, default: nil)\n74\t  attr(:label, :string, default: nil, doc: \"Override the default auto-generated label\")\n75\t\n76\t  def trace_map_bubble(assigns) do\n77\t    assigns =\n78\t      assigns\n79\t      |> assign_new(:label, fn -> default_label(assigns.dimension, assigns.value) end)\n80\t      |> assign_new(:id, fn -> bubble_dom_id(assigns.dimension, assigns.value) end)\n81\t\n82\t    ~H\"\"\"\n83\t    <button\n84\t      type=\"button\"\n85\t      id={@id}\n86\t      data-testid=\"trace-map-bubble\"\n87\t      data-bubble-dimension={to_string(@dimension)}\n88\t      data-bubble-value={@value}\n89\t      data-bubble-count={to_string(@count)}\n90\t      data-bubble-off={to_string(@off)}\n91\t      aria-pressed={to_string(@active)}\n92\t      aria-label={aria_label(@mode, @dimension, @value, @off)}\n93\t      title={off_title(@off, @dimension, @value)}\n94\t      disabled={@mode == :readonly}\n95\t      phx-click={if @mode != :readonly, do: @on_click}\n96\t      class={[\n97\t        \"btn btn-sm gap-1 normal-case\",\n98\t        @off && \"opacity-60\",\n99\t        @active && not @off && \"btn-active\",\n100\t        (not @active or @off) && \"btn-outline\"\n101\t      ]}\n102\t    ><span>{@label}</span><span :if={@off} class=\"text-xs\">⚫ off</span><span class=\"badge badge-sm\">{@count}</span></button>\n103\t    \"\"\"\n104\t  end\n105\t\n106\t  @doc false\n107\t  def bubble_dom_id(dimension, value) do\n108\t    \"trace-bubble-\" <> Atom.to_string(dimension) <> \"-\" <> slug(value)\n109\t  end\n110\t\n111\t  defp slug(value) do\n112\t    value\n113\t    |> to_string()\n114\t    |> String.replace(~r/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/, \"-\")\n115\t  end\n116\t\n117\t  defp default_label(:level, value),\n118\t    do: level_icon(value) <> \" \" <> String.upcase(to_string(value))\n119\t\n120\t  defp default_label(_dimension, value), do: to_string(value)\n121\t\n122\t  defp level_icon(\"error\"), do: \"🔴\"\n123\t  defp level_icon(\"warning\"), do: \"🟠\"\n124\t  defp level_icon(\"info\"), do: \"🟢\"\n125\t  defp level_icon(\"debug\"), do: \"⚪\"\n126\t  defp level_icon(_), do: \"⚪\"\n127\t\n128\t  defp aria_label(:configure, dimension, value, _off) do\n129\t    \"Configure retention for #{dimension_label(dimension)} #{value}\"\n130\t  end\n131\t\n132\t  # AC8/amendment 3: a :readonly bubble is a live-count LEGEND, never a\n133\t  # filter — its accessible name must never use the :filter mode's \"Filter\n134\t  # by ...\" wording, which would misleadingly claim interactivity.\n135\t  defp aria_label(:readonly, dimension, value, _off) do\n136\t    \"#{String.capitalize(dimension_label(dimension))} #{value}, live count\"\n137\t  end\n138\t\n139\t  # V5(a) fix (UX/accessibility gate, BLOCKING): a visual \"⚫ off\" marker and a\n140\t  # hover-only `title` tooltip communicate the \"not currently collected\"\n141\t  # (structural absence, AC2) state to sighted mouse users only. A screen\n142\t  # reader announcing this button's accessible name previously heard the\n143\t  # exact same generic \"Filter by debug level\" whether or not debug was being\n144\t  # collected — the not-collected state must be in the aria-label itself.\n145\t  defp aria_label(_mode, dimension, value, true) do\n146\t    \"Filter by #{value} #{dimension_label(dimension)} — not currently collected\"\n147\t  end\n148\t\n149\t  defp aria_label(_mode, dimension, value, _off) do\n150\t    \"Filter by #{value} #{dimension_label(dimension)}\"\n151\t  end\n152\t\n153\t  defp dimension_label(:endpoint), do: \"endpoint\"\n154\t  defp dimension_label(:level), do: \"level\"\n155\t  defp dimension_label(:source), do: \"source\"\n156\t  defp dimension_label(other), do: to_string(other)\n157\t\n158\t  defp off_title(false, _dimension, _value), do: nil\n159\t\n160\t  defp off_title(true, dimension, value) do\n161\t    \"#{String.capitalize(dimension_label(dimension))} #{value} is not currently collected\"\n162\t  end\n163\tend\n164\t"}]},"uuid":"40d1b731-d4c5-4fcf-a2a8-64ea3d8727b4","timestamp":"2026-07-08T14:02:37.621Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_bubble.ex","content":"defmodule Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapBubble do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  Reusable Trace Map \"bubble\" component (enhanced-logging/admin-logs-monitoring-view,\n  AC1/AC2/AC5/AC13/AC14/AC16).\n\n  A single toggleable pill-shaped button that is BOTH legend (shows a live count)\n  and interactive filter. Renders identically regardless of `mode` — the component\n  itself has NO opinion on what clicking it does server-side; it simply wires\n  whatever `on_click` value the caller passes onto the rendered `<button>`'s\n  `phx-click` attribute. That is the entire \"designed for reuse\" contract (see\n  `test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_bubble_test.exs`, Acceptance Test 11):\n\n    * `mode: :filter` (THIS story, `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`) — the caller passes a\n      `Phoenix.LiveView.JS` command list as `on_click` (client-side DOM toggle +\n      a `nebu:trace-bubble-click` custom-event dispatch picked up by\n      `priv/static/assets/app.js` — see that file's `wireTraceMapFilters`).\n      AC3 requires this to be genuinely client-side-only (no `handle_event`\n      server round-trip per click), so `on_click` here is NEVER a bare event-name\n      string in real `LogsLive` usage.\n    * `mode: :configure` (sibling `admin-logs-retention-settings` story, REUSING\n      this same component) — that story passes a bare event-name string as\n      `on_click` (a normal `phx-click=\"configure_retention\"` push), since\n      AC3's client-side-only requirement is scoped to `:filter` mode only.\n    * `mode: :readonly` (enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page, AC8 +\n      amendment 3 — REUSING this same component for the War Room scoping\n      form's live-activity preview, level+source dimensions only) — the\n      bubble is a live-count LEGEND, never a filter: it renders as a\n      genuinely non-interactive, `disabled` `<button>` with NO `phx-click`\n      attribute at all, regardless of what `on_click` value the caller\n      passes (the component itself refuses to wire it up in this mode,\n      rather than merely trusting the caller to pass `on_click: nil`). The\n      accessible name never uses the `:filter` mode's \"Filter by ...\"\n      wording (that phrasing is reserved for a genuinely interactive\n      bubble).\n\n  ## Accessibility (AC13/AC14)\n\n  Every bubble is a native `<button>` (natural Tab order, Space/Enter activation),\n  carries `aria-pressed` (true/false) and a descriptive `aria-label` — never a\n  `<div onclick>`. Level indication pairs an icon with text (never color alone).\n\n  ## \"Off\" (uncollected) bubbles (AC2)\n\n  When `off: true`, the bubble renders a distinct \"⚫ off\" visual marker\n  (`data-bubble-off=\"true\"`) instead of a live count — this represents a\n  STRUCTURAL absence (the level/source is not currently being collected per the\n  retention policy), not \"zero events in the active window\".\n  \"\"\"\n  use Phoenix.Component\n\n  attr(:dimension, :atom, required: true, doc: \"One of :endpoint, :level, :source\")\n\n  attr(:value, :string,\n    required: true,\n    doc: \"The specific value, e.g. \\\"/login\\\", \\\"error\\\", \\\"ecto\\\"\"\n  )\n\n  attr(:count, :integer, required: true, doc: \"Live count of matching events in the current view\")\n  attr(:active, :boolean, default: false, doc: \"Whether this filter is currently active\")\n  attr(:mode, :atom, default: :filter, values: [:filter, :configure, :readonly])\n\n  attr(:on_click, :any,\n    required: true,\n    doc:\n      \"phx-click value: a Phoenix.LiveView.JS command list (:filter) or a bare event name (:configure)\"\n  )\n\n  attr(:off, :boolean,\n    default: false,\n    doc: \"True when this dimension value is not currently collected (AC2)\"\n  )\n\n  attr(:id, :string, default: nil)\n  attr(:label, :string, default: nil, doc: \"Override the default auto-generated label\")\n\n  def trace_map_bubble(assigns) do\n    assigns =\n      assigns\n      |> assign_new(:label, fn -> default_label(assigns.dimension, assigns.value) end)\n      |> assign_new(:id, fn -> bubble_dom_id(assigns.dimension, assigns.value) end)\n\n    ~H\"\"\"\n    <button\n      type=\"button\"\n      id={@id}\n      data-testid=\"trace-map-bubble\"\n      data-bubble-dimension={to_string(@dimension)}\n      data-bubble-value={@value}\n      data-bubble-count={to_string(@count)}\n      data-bubble-off={to_string(@off)}\n      aria-pressed={to_string(@active)}\n      aria-label={aria_label(@mode, @dimension, @value, @off)}\n      title={off_title(@off, @dimension, @value)}\n      disabled={@mode == :readonly}\n      phx-click={if @mode != :readonly, do: @on_click}\n      class={[\n        \"btn btn-sm gap-1 normal-case\",\n        @off && \"opacity-60\",\n        @active && not @off && \"btn-active\",\n        (not @active or @off) && \"btn-outline\"\n      ]}\n    ><span>{@label}</span><span :if={@off} class=\"text-xs\">⚫ off</span><span class=\"badge badge-sm\">{@count}</span></button>\n    \"\"\"\n  end\n\n  @doc false\n  def bubble_dom_id(dimension, value) do\n    \"trace-bubble-\" <> Atom.to_string(dimension) <> \"-\" <> slug(value)\n  end\n\n  defp slug(value) do\n    value\n    |> to_string()\n    |> String.replace(~r/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/, \"-\")\n  end\n\n  defp default_label(:level, value),\n    do: level_icon(value) <> \" \" <> String.upcase(to_string(value))\n\n  defp default_label(_dimension, value), do: to_string(value)\n\n  defp level_icon(\"error\"), do: \"🔴\"\n  defp level_icon(\"warning\"), do: \"🟠\"\n  defp level_icon(\"info\"), do: \"🟢\"\n  defp level_icon(\"debug\"), do: \"⚪\"\n  defp level_icon(_), do: \"⚪\"\n\n  defp aria_label(:configure, dimension, value, _off) do\n    \"Configure retention for #{dimension_label(dimension)} #{value}\"\n  end\n\n  # AC8/amendment 3: a :readonly bubble is a live-count LEGEND, never a\n  # filter — its accessible name must never use the :filter mode's \"Filter\n  # by ...\" wording, which would misleadingly claim interactivity.\n  defp aria_label(:readonly, dimension, value, _off) do\n    \"#{String.capitalize(dimension_label(dimension))} #{value}, live count\"\n  end\n\n  # V5(a) fix (UX/accessibility gate, BLOCKING): a visual \"⚫ off\" marker and a\n  # hover-only `title` tooltip communicate the \"not currently collected\"\n  # (structural absence, AC2) state to sighted mouse users only. A screen\n  # reader announcing this button's accessible name previously heard the\n  # exact same generic \"Filter by debug level\" whether or not debug was being\n  # collected — the not-collected state must be in the aria-label itself.\n  defp aria_label(_mode, dimension, value, true) do\n    \"Filter by #{value} #{dimension_label(dimension)} — not currently collected\"\n  end\n\n  defp aria_label(_mode, dimension, value, _off) do\n    \"Filter by #{value} #{dimension_label(dimension)}\"\n  end\n\n  defp dimension_label(:endpoint), do: \"endpoint\"\n  defp dimension_label(:level), do: \"level\"\n  defp dimension_label(:source), do: \"source\"\n  defp dimension_label(other), do: to_string(other)\n\n  defp off_title(false, _dimension, _value), do: nil\n\n  defp off_title(true, dimension, value) do\n    \"#{String.capitalize(dimension_label(dimension))} #{value} is not currently collected\"\n  end\nend\n","numLines":164,"startLine":1,"totalLines":164}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"21ececdc-b22a-4a85-970b-c159d3f2ba9a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"8f08c0bf-6e3e-4369-b0b7-cef55c76c401","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ac24982ebd356bd9e","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5","id":"msg_011CcpiS36s2YLdFpJX3YYtK","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01MwK1T5Khi3CpfLeMM8pXX1","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/war_room_test.exs","offset":1,"limit":50},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":452,"cache_read_input_tokens":94263,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":452,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":2,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CcpiS21t6rNNn1VgTwXrR","attributionAgent":"nebu-ux-reviewer","type":"assistant","uuid":"9414b6e6-34dc-4c54-ac6e-3644e596feff","timestamp":"2026-07-08T14:03:41.273Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}
{"parentUuid":"9414b6e6-34dc-4c54-ac6e-3644e596feff","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"f289eda1-327c-4f45-b999-620f23fc1caf","agentId":"ac24982ebd356bd9e","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MwK1T5Khi3CpfLeMM8pXX1","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tdefmodule Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoomTest do\n2\t  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n3\t  RED-phase ATDD tests for `enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page`\n4\t  (AC1-AC14, as corrected by the story's \"Story Amendments (2026-07-08)\"\n5\t  section — the amendments are the source of truth wherever they conflict\n6\t  with the story's original literal text).\n7\t\n8\t  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page.md\n9\t\n10\t  ## Architecture this file pins down (amendment 1)\n11\t\n12\t  A SINGLE new LiveView module, `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoom`, mirroring the\n13\t  sibling `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`'s own \"one module, two router\n14\t  actions\" shape (see that module's moduledoc for why this is a SEPARATE\n15\t  module from the existing `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`, not a bolted-on action —\n16\t  the identical `live_isolated/3` + `handle_params/3` incompatibility\n17\t  reasoning would apply here too if this module needed `handle_params/3`;\n18\t  it does NOT — see below):\n19\t\n20\t      live \"/logs/war-room\",           LogsLive.WarRoom, :index\n21\t      live \"/logs/war-room/:room_id\",  LogsLive.WarRoom, :show\n22\t\n23\t  `@active_nav` is `\"logs-war-room\"` (matching the ALREADY-SHIPPED sidebar\n24\t  child key from `admin-sidebar-nesting` — see `layouts.ex`'s\n25\t  `@logs_nav_children`) in BOTH actions.\n26\t\n27\t  ## Why `handle_params/3` is NOT needed here (unlike TraceMap)\n28\t\n29\t  `:show`'s `room_id` is a static PATH segment, available directly in\n30\t  `mount/3`'s own `params` argument (`%{\"room_id\" => id}`) — no\n31\t  `push_patch/2`-driven URL round-trip is needed the way TraceMap's\n32\t  bracket-array endpoint filters need one. The exclusive-scope checkbox\n33\t  toggling (AC9) and scope-mode switching (AC8) are ordinary\n34\t  `phx-change`/assigns state, not URL-driven. This means `mount/3` can\n35\t  branch on `params[\"room_id\"]` (present -> detail mode, absent/\n36\t  `:not_mounted_at_router` -> list mode) directly, exactly like the\n37\t  EXISTING `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s own `war_room_id_param/1` precedent —\n38\t  and therefore `live_isolated/3` CAN mount this module for every list-mode\n39\t  test below (no `handle_params/3` export to trip the\n40\t  `live_isolated/3`-breaks-on-`handle_params/3` incompatibility TraceMap's\n41\t  moduledoc documents). Only the DETAIL view (`:show`, needs a real path\n42\t  param) and the AC12 route-repoint proof need the real router.\n43\t\n44\t  ## Why these MUST FAIL right now\n45\t\n46\t  `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoom` does not exist at all — every\n47\t  `live_isolated/3`/`live/1` call below fails with `UndefinedFunctionError`\n48\t  (list-mode tests) or the real router still dispatches\n49\t  `/admin/logs/war-room` to the OLD stub (`LogsLive, :index` — literally the\n50\t  Live Stream page, mislabeled per amendment 1) for the router-mounted"}]},"uuid":"05e13223-362f-451d-938f-a5c19a8e6e57","timestamp":"2026-07-08T14:03:41.306Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/war_room_test.exs","content":"defmodule Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoomTest do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  RED-phase ATDD tests for `enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page`\n  (AC1-AC14, as corrected by the story's \"Story Amendments (2026-07-08)\"\n  section — the amendments are the source of truth wherever they conflict\n  with the story's original literal text).\n\n  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page.md\n\n  ## Architecture this file pins down (amendment 1)\n\n  A SINGLE new LiveView module, `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoom`, mirroring the\n  sibling `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap`'s own \"one module, two router\n  actions\" shape (see that module's moduledoc for why this is a SEPARATE\n  module from the existing `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`, not a bolted-on action —\n  the identical `live_isolated/3` + `handle_params/3` incompatibility\n  reasoning would apply here too if this module needed `handle_params/3`;\n  it does NOT — see below):\n\n      live \"/logs/war-room\",           LogsLive.WarRoom, :index\n      live \"/logs/war-room/:room_id\",  LogsLive.WarRoom, :show\n\n  `@active_nav` is `\"logs-war-room\"` (matching the ALREADY-SHIPPED sidebar\n  child key from `admin-sidebar-nesting` — see `layouts.ex`'s\n  `@logs_nav_children`) in BOTH actions.\n\n  ## Why `handle_params/3` is NOT needed here (unlike TraceMap)\n\n  `:show`'s `room_id` is a static PATH segment, available directly in\n  `mount/3`'s own `params` argument (`%{\"room_id\" => id}`) — no\n  `push_patch/2`-driven URL round-trip is needed the way TraceMap's\n  bracket-array endpoint filters need one. The exclusive-scope checkbox\n  toggling (AC9) and scope-mode switching (AC8) are ordinary\n  `phx-change`/assigns state, not URL-driven. This means `mount/3` can\n  branch on `params[\"room_id\"]` (present -> detail mode, absent/\n  `:not_mounted_at_router` -> list mode) directly, exactly like the\n  EXISTING `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s own `war_room_id_param/1` precedent —\n  and therefore `live_isolated/3` CAN mount this module for every list-mode\n  test below (no `handle_params/3` export to trip the\n  `live_isolated/3`-breaks-on-`handle_params/3` incompatibility TraceMap's\n  moduledoc documents). Only the DETAIL view (`:show`, needs a real path\n  param) and the AC12 route-repoint proof need the real router.\n\n  ## Why these MUST FAIL right now\n\n  `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoom` does not exist at all — every\n  `live_isolated/3`/`live/1` call below fails with `UndefinedFunctionError`\n  (list-mode tests) or the real router still dispatches\n  `/admin/logs/war-room` to the OLD stub (`LogsLive, :index` — literally the\n  Live Stream page, mislabeled per amendment 1) for the router-mounted","numLines":50,"startLine":1,"totalLines":1076}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"9414b6e6-34dc-4c54-ac6e-3644e596feff","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

Mirrors wireLogsConfigToggles()'s\n  // exact CSP-`script-src 'self'`-compliant pattern (no inline <script>).\n  function wireWarRoomPanel() {\n    if (window.__nebuWarRoomWired) return;\n    window.__nebuWarRoomWired = true;\n\n    document.addEventListener(\"change\", function (event) {\n      var select = event.target.closest\n        ? event.target.closest(\n            '[data-testid=\"war-room-start-form\"] select[name=\"scope\"]',\n          )\n        : null;\n      if (!select) return;\n\n      var warningAll = document.querySelector('[data-testid=\"war-room-warning-all\"]');\n      var warningSync = document.querySelector('[data-testid=\"war-room-warning-sync\"]');\n\n      // code-review MINOR-1 (war-room-mode, cycle 1): the default/empty \"\"\n      // select value (the \"-- select scope --\" placeholder option) is the\n      // SAME null-scope case as an explicit \"all\" per AC7/AC3 (Dev Notes:\n      // \"NULL, treated as 'all'\") — the broadest, most dangerous case\n      // (collecting everything). Treat \"\" identically to \"all\" here so an\n      // operator who never touches the dropdown still sees the warning\n      // once they interact with the form at all.\n      if (select.value === \"all\" || select.value === \"\") {\n        if (warningAll) warningAll.style.display = \"\";\n        if (warningSync) warningSync.style.display = \"none\";\n      } else if (select.value === \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\") {\n        if (warningAll) warningAll.style.display = \"none\";\n        if (warningSync) warningSync.style.display = \"\";\n      } else {\n        if (warningAll) warningAll.style.display = \"none\";\n        if (warningSync) warningSync.style.display = \"none\";\n      }\n    });\n  }\n\n  // enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand (AC7/AC9/AC10/AC11): all client-only\n  // behavior for the /admin/logs/trace-map Leitstand page, wired as a single\n  // phx-hook on the page's root element (CSP is `script-src 'self'` — no\n  // inline JS anywhere; every listener below is added via addEventListener\n  // from this external file):\n  //   - Enter/Space keyboard activation for the SVG map nodes (AC10 — an SVG\n  //     <g role=\"button\" tabindex=\"0\"> has NO native keyboard activation, so\n  //     keydown is translated into the same bubbling click phx-click handles)","numLines":60,"startLine":1160,"totalLines":1599}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d1ddcf5a-4d84-4aa7-808c-5fc3f8fb9e5a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"35e69516-598c-4261-99ae-fb3c92515140","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"f289eda1-327c-4f45-b999-620f23fc1caf","agentId":"ac24982ebd356bd9e","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011SCKENqu8oLRaqvE7AWEAN","type":"tool_result","content":"defmodule Nebu.Admin.LayoutsWarRoomIndicatorTest do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  RED-phase ATDD tests for `enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page`,\n  Story Amendment 4 (2026-07-08) — a pulsing recording dot on the sidebar's\n  \"War Room\" nav item, and a recording pill in the topbar, while a War Room\n  session is active. This closes the gap left after `trace-map-leitstand`\n  shipped only the Trace Map page's OWN in-page banner: an operator on ANY\n  other admin page (Dashboard, Users, Rooms, ...) previously had no way to\n  tell a session was running without navigating to `/admin/logs` or\n  `/admin/logs/trace-map` first.\n\n  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page.md\n  Approved visual reference (amendment 4's own citation):\n  `_bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-dummy.html`\n  — topbar `.pill--warroom` (`\"War Room recording · \" <> mm:ss`) and sidebar\n  `#wr-navdot`.\n\n  ## Why these MUST FAIL right now\n\n  `Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1` (server/lib/nebu/admin/layouts.ex) has no\n  `:war_room_active`/`:war_room_session` assign at all today — the topbar\n  `<header>` renders only the static \"Nebu Admin\" brand text, and the\n  sidebar's \"Logs\" section's children (`@logs_nav_children`, including the\n  already-shipped `\"logs-war-room\"` key from `admin-sidebar-nesting`) render\n  via the existing, dot-agnostic `nav_item/1` child markup. Every test below\n  fails because neither `[data-testid=\"nav-war-room-dot\"]` nor\n  `[data-testid=\"topbar-war-room-pill\"]` exist in the rendered markup at\n  all, regardless of the assign passed in.\n\n  ## Contract pinned by this file\n\n    * `Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1` accepts new assigns (safely defaulted via\n      `assign_new/3` for every OTHER existing LiveView call site that does\n      not set them, mirroring the existing `:active_nav`/\n      `:bootstrap_completed` precedent):\n        - `:war_room_active` (boolean, default `false`)\n        - `:war_room_started_at` (`DateTime.t() | nil`, default `nil`) —\n          used to compute the topbar pill's elapsed `mm:ss` text\n        - `:war_room_started_by` (`String.t() | nil`, default `nil`) —\n          optional, for a fuller pill label\n      Amendment 4's own wording: \"sourced from\n      `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.status/0`, polled the same way\n      `LogsLive`/`LogsLive.TraceMap` already do\" — this file pins the\n      RENDER contract only; the poll wiring itself is exercised end-to-end\n      by the `@describetag :integration` block below and by\n      `e2e/features/admin/war-room-dedicated-page.feature`.\n    * Sidebar: `[data-testid=\"nav-war-room-dot\"]` renders inside the\n      `<li>` for `[data-navkey=\"logs-war-room\"]` ONLY when\n      `@war_room_active` is `true`; absent when `false` (the default).\n    * Topbar: `[data-testid=\"topbar-war-room-pill\"]` in the `<header>`\n      renders ONLY when `@war_room_active` is `true`; absent when `false`.\n      When present, it contains the text \"War Room recording\" (per the\n      approved dummy's `.pill--warroom` copy) — the exact `mm:ss` ticking\n      value is client-side/E2E territory (mirrors\n      `trace-map-war-room-elapsed`'s own `data-started-at` + app.js-tick\n      precedent), so this file only pins a `data-started-at` attribute\n      carrying the session's start time for that same client-side tick\n      mechanism to consume.\n    * Both indicators are ADDITIVE — the pre-existing \"Nebu Admin\" brand\n      text and the \"Logs\" section's other children render unchanged\n      regardless of `@war_room_active`.\n  \"\"\"\n  use ExUnit.Case, async: false\n\n  import Plug.Test\n  import Plug.Conn\n  import Phoenix.LiveViewTest\n  import Phoenix.ConnTest\n\n  @endpoint Nebu.Admin.Endpoint\n  @secret_key_base String.duplicate(\"nebu-wr-indicator-test-key\", 2)\n\n  defp find(html, selector), do: html |> Floki.find(selector) |> List.first()\n  defp attr(node, name), do: node |> List.wrap() |> Floki.attribute(name) |> List.first()\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Component-level contract (no DB, no router) — mirrors\n  # trace_map_bubble_test.exs's render_component/2 pattern.\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"amendment 4: Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1 renders the dot/pill from assigns\" do\n    defp base_assigns(overrides) do\n      Map.merge(\n        %{\n          __changed__: nil,\n          active_nav: \"dashboard\",\n          bootstrap_completed: true,\n          inner_content: \"content\",\n          war_room_active: false,\n          war_room_started_at: nil,\n          war_room_started_by: nil\n        },\n        overrides\n      )\n    end\n\n    test \"with war_room_active: false (default), neither the nav dot nor the topbar pill render\" do\n      html = render_component(&Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1, base_assigns(%{})) |> Floki.parse_document!()\n\n      refute find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"nav-war-room-dot\"])),\n             \"amendment 4: no session active -> no sidebar recording dot\"\n\n      refute find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"topbar-war-room-pill\"])),\n             \"amendment 4: no session active -> no topbar recording pill\"\n    end\n\n    test \"with war_room_active: true, the sidebar recording dot renders on the War Room nav item\" do\n      html =\n        render_component(\n          &Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1,\n          base_assigns(%{\n            war_room_active: true,\n            war_room_started_at: ~U[2026-07-08 10:00:00Z],\n            war_room_started_by: \"@admin:test.local\"\n          })\n        )\n        |> Floki.parse_document!()\n\n      war_room_li =\n        html\n        |> Floki.find(~s(li))\n        |> Enum.find(fn li -> Floki.find(li, ~s([data-navkey=\"logs-war-room\"])) != [] end)\n\n      assert war_room_li, \"expected the 'War Room' nav item (data-navkey=\\\"logs-war-room\\\") to exist\"\n\n      assert Floki.find(war_room_li, ~s([data-testid=\"nav-war-room-dot\"])) != [],\n             \"amendment 4: an active session must show a recording dot on the sidebar's \" <>\n               \"War Room nav item\"\n    end\n\n    test \"with war_room_active: true, the topbar recording pill renders with the expected copy\" do\n      html =\n        render_component(\n          &Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1,\n          base_assigns(%{\n            war_room_active: true,\n            war_room_started_at: ~U[2026-07-08 10:00:00Z],\n            war_room_started_by: \"@admin:test.local\"\n          })\n        )\n        |> Floki.parse_document!()\n\n      pill = find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"topbar-war-room-pill\"]))\n      assert pill, \"amendment 4: expected a topbar recording pill while a session is active\"\n\n      assert Floki.text(pill) =~ \"War Room recording\",\n             \"amendment 4: the pill copy must match the approved dummy's \" <>\n               \"'War Room recording · mm:ss' wording\"\n\n      assert attr(pill, \"data-started-at\"),\n             \"amendment 4: the pill must carry a data-started-at attribute for the \" <>\n               \"client-side elapsed-time tick (mirrors trace-map-war-room-elapsed)\"\n    end\n\n    test \"other Logs children and the brand text are unaffected by war_room_active\" do\n      html =\n        render_component(\n          &Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1,\n          base_assigns(%{war_room_active: true, war_room_started_at: ~U[2026-07-08 10:00:00Z]})\n        )\n        |> Floki.parse_document!()\n\n      assert Floki.find(html, ~s([data-navkey=\"logs-monitoring\"])) != [],\n             \"amendment 4: unrelated sidebar children must render unchanged\"\n\n      assert Floki.text(html) =~ \"Nebu Admin\",\n             \"amendment 4: the topbar brand text must remain, alongside the pill\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Full-stack integration — a genuinely active session (DB-backed)\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"amendment 4: a real active session shows the dot/pill on an UNRELATED admin page\" do\n    @describetag :integration\n\n    defp admin_conn(path) do\n      %{conn(:get, path) | secret_key_base: @secret_key_base}\n      |> @endpoint.admin_session([])\n      |> fetch_session()\n      |> put_session(\"current_user\", %{\n        \"admin\" => true,\n        \"sub\" => \"admin-wr-indicator-tests\",\n        \"matrix_user_id\" => \"@admin:test.local\"\n      })\n    end\n\n    setup do\n      Nebu.Release.migrate()\n\n      Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query(\n        Nebu.Repo,\n        \"UPDATE war_room_sessions SET ended_at = now(), ended_reason = 'manual_stop' WHERE ended_at IS NULL\",\n        []\n      )\n\n      :ok\n    end\n\n    test \"visible from /admin/logs (an unrelated admin LiveView page), not just the War Room page itself\" do\n      {:ok, session} =\n        Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session(%{\n          started_by: \"@wr-indicator-tests:nebu.example\",\n          reason: \"amendment 4 dot/pill ATDD probe\",\n          max_entries: 100,\n          max_duration_s: 300\n        })\n\n      on_exit(fn -> Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.stop_session(session.id) end)\n\n      sent_conn = \"/admin/logs\" |> admin_conn() |> @endpoint.call(@endpoint.init([]))\n      {:ok, _view, html} = live(sent_conn)\n      doc = Floki.parse_document!(html)\n\n      assert find(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"nav-war-room-dot\"])),\n             \"amendment 4: 'visible from anywhere' — an active session must show the sidebar \" <>\n               \"dot even while viewing the UNRELATED /admin/logs page, not only the dedicated \" <>\n               \"/admin/logs/war-room page\"\n\n      assert find(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"topbar-war-room-pill\"])),\n             \"amendment 4: the topbar pill must likewise be visible from /admin/logs\"\n    end\n  end\nend","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"53e2baca-d07c-486d-ae0a-838b785123da","timestamp":"2026-07-08T14:05:12.810Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"defmodule Nebu.Admin.LayoutsWarRoomIndicatorTest do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  RED-phase ATDD tests for `enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page`,\n  Story Amendment 4 (2026-07-08) — a pulsing recording dot on the sidebar's\n  \"War Room\" nav item, and a recording pill in the topbar, while a War Room\n  session is active. This closes the gap left after `trace-map-leitstand`\n  shipped only the Trace Map page's OWN in-page banner: an operator on ANY\n  other admin page (Dashboard, Users, Rooms, ...) previously had no way to\n  tell a session was running without navigating to `/admin/logs` or\n  `/admin/logs/trace-map` first.\n\n  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/war-room-dedicated-page.md\n  Approved visual reference (amendment 4's own citation):\n  `_bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-dummy.html`\n  — topbar `.pill--warroom` (`\"War Room recording · \" <> mm:ss`) and sidebar\n  `#wr-navdot`.\n\n  ## Why these MUST FAIL right now\n\n  `Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1` (server/lib/nebu/admin/layouts.ex) has no\n  `:war_room_active`/`:war_room_session` assign at all today — the topbar\n  `<header>` renders only the static \"Nebu Admin\" brand text, and the\n  sidebar's \"Logs\" section's children (`@logs_nav_children`, including the\n  already-shipped `\"logs-war-room\"` key from `admin-sidebar-nesting`) render\n  via the existing, dot-agnostic `nav_item/1` child markup. Every test below\n  fails because neither `[data-testid=\"nav-war-room-dot\"]` nor\n  `[data-testid=\"topbar-war-room-pill\"]` exist in the rendered markup at\n  all, regardless of the assign passed in.\n\n  ## Contract pinned by this file\n\n    * `Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1` accepts new assigns (safely defaulted via\n      `assign_new/3` for every OTHER existing LiveView call site that does\n      not set them, mirroring the existing `:active_nav`/\n      `:bootstrap_completed` precedent):\n        - `:war_room_active` (boolean, default `false`)\n        - `:war_room_started_at` (`DateTime.t() | nil`, default `nil`) —\n          used to compute the topbar pill's elapsed `mm:ss` text\n        - `:war_room_started_by` (`String.t() | nil`, default `nil`) —\n          optional, for a fuller pill label\n      Amendment 4's own wording: \"sourced from\n      `Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.status/0`, polled the same way\n      `LogsLive`/`LogsLive.TraceMap` already do\" — this file pins the\n      RENDER contract only; the poll wiring itself is exercised end-to-end\n      by the `@describetag :integration` block below and by\n      `e2e/features/admin/war-room-dedicated-page.feature`.\n    * Sidebar: `[data-testid=\"nav-war-room-dot\"]` renders inside the\n      `<li>` for `[data-navkey=\"logs-war-room\"]` ONLY when\n      `@war_room_active` is `true`; absent when `false` (the default).\n    * Topbar: `[data-testid=\"topbar-war-room-pill\"]` in the `<header>`\n      renders ONLY when `@war_room_active` is `true`; absent when `false`.\n      When present, it contains the text \"War Room recording\" (per the\n      approved dummy's `.pill--warroom` copy) — the exact `mm:ss` ticking\n      value is client-side/E2E territory (mirrors\n      `trace-map-war-room-elapsed`'s own `data-started-at` + app.js-tick\n      precedent), so this file only pins a `data-started-at` attribute\n      carrying the session's start time for that same client-side tick\n      mechanism to consume.\n    * Both indicators are ADDITIVE — the pre-existing \"Nebu Admin\" brand\n      text and the \"Logs\" section's other children render unchanged\n      regardless of `@war_room_active`.\n  \"\"\"\n  use ExUnit.Case, async: false\n\n  import Plug.Test\n  import Plug.Conn\n  import Phoenix.LiveViewTest\n  import Phoenix.ConnTest\n\n  @endpoint Nebu.Admin.Endpoint\n  @secret_key_base String.duplicate(\"nebu-wr-indicator-test-key\", 2)\n\n  defp find(html, selector), do: html |> Floki.find(selector) |> List.first()\n  defp attr(node, name), do: node |> List.wrap() |> Floki.attribute(name) |> List.first()\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Component-level contract (no DB, no router) — mirrors\n  # trace_map_bubble_test.exs's render_component/2 pattern.\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"amendment 4: Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1 renders the dot/pill from assigns\" do\n    defp base_assigns(overrides) do\n      Map.merge(\n        %{\n          __changed__: nil,\n          active_nav: \"dashboard\",\n          bootstrap_completed: true,\n          inner_content: \"content\",\n          war_room_active: false,\n          war_room_started_at: nil,\n          war_room_started_by: nil\n        },\n        overrides\n      )\n    end\n\n    test \"with war_room_active: false (default), neither the nav dot nor the topbar pill render\" do\n      html = render_component(&Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1, base_assigns(%{})) |> Floki.parse_document!()\n\n      refute find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"nav-war-room-dot\"])),\n             \"amendment 4: no session active -> no sidebar recording dot\"\n\n      refute find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"topbar-war-room-pill\"])),\n             \"amendment 4: no session active -> no topbar recording pill\"\n    end\n\n    test \"with war_room_active: true, the sidebar recording dot renders on the War Room nav item\" do\n      html =\n        render_component(\n          &Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1,\n          base_assigns(%{\n            war_room_active: true,\n            war_room_started_at: ~U[2026-07-08 10:00:00Z],\n            war_room_started_by: \"@admin:test.local\"\n          })\n        )\n        |> Floki.parse_document!()\n\n      war_room_li =\n        html\n        |> Floki.find(~s(li))\n        |> Enum.find(fn li -> Floki.find(li, ~s([data-navkey=\"logs-war-room\"])) != [] end)\n\n      assert war_room_li, \"expected the 'War Room' nav item (data-navkey=\\\"logs-war-room\\\") to exist\"\n\n      assert Floki.find(war_room_li, ~s([data-testid=\"nav-war-room-dot\"])) != [],\n             \"amendment 4: an active session must show a recording dot on the sidebar's \" <>\n               \"War Room nav item\"\n    end\n\n    test \"with war_room_active: true, the topbar recording pill renders with the expected copy\" do\n      html =\n        render_component(\n          &Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1,\n          base_assigns(%{\n            war_room_active: true,\n            war_room_started_at: ~U[2026-07-08 10:00:00Z],\n            war_room_started_by: \"@admin:test.local\"\n          })\n        )\n        |> Floki.parse_document!()\n\n      pill = find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"topbar-war-room-pill\"]))\n      assert pill, \"amendment 4: expected a topbar recording pill while a session is active\"\n\n      assert Floki.text(pill) =~ \"War Room recording\",\n             \"amendment 4: the pill copy must match the approved dummy's \" <>\n               \"'War Room recording · mm:ss' wording\"\n\n      assert attr(pill, \"data-started-at\"),\n             \"amendment 4: the pill must carry a data-started-at attribute for the \" <>\n               \"client-side elapsed-time tick (mirrors trace-map-war-room-elapsed)\"\n    end\n\n    test \"other Logs children and the brand text are unaffected by war_room_active\" do\n      html =\n        render_component(\n          &Nebu.Admin.Layouts.live/1,\n          base_assigns(%{war_room_active: true, war_room_started_at: ~U[2026-07-08 10:00:00Z]})\n        )\n        |> Floki.parse_document!()\n\n      assert Floki.find(html, ~s([data-navkey=\"logs-monitoring\"])) != [],\n             \"amendment 4: unrelated sidebar children must render unchanged\"\n\n      assert Floki.text(html) =~ \"Nebu Admin\",\n             \"amendment 4: the topbar brand text must remain, alongside the pill\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # Full-stack integration — a genuinely active session (DB-backed)\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"amendment 4: a real active session shows the dot/pill on an UNRELATED admin page\" do\n    @describetag :integration\n\n    defp admin_conn(path) do\n      %{conn(:get, path) | secret_key_base: @secret_key_base}\n      |> @endpoint.admin_session([])\n      |> fetch_session()\n      |> put_session(\"current_user\", %{\n        \"admin\" => true,\n        \"sub\" => \"admin-wr-indicator-tests\",\n        \"matrix_user_id\" => \"@admin:test.local\"\n      })\n    end\n\n    setup do\n      Nebu.Release.migrate()\n\n      Ecto.Adapters.SQL.query(\n        Nebu.Repo,\n        \"UPDATE war_room_sessions SET ended_at = now(), ended_reason = 'manual_stop' WHERE ended_at IS NULL\",\n        []\n      )\n\n      :ok\n    end\n\n    test \"visible from /admin/logs (an unrelated admin LiveView page), not just the War Room page itself\" do\n      {:ok, session} =\n        Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session(%{\n          started_by: \"@wr-indicator-tests:nebu.example\",\n          reason: \"amendment 4 dot/pill ATDD probe\",\n          max_entries: 100,\n          max_duration_s: 300\n        })\n\n      on_exit(fn -> Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.stop_session(session.id) end)\n\n      sent_conn = \"/admin/logs\" |> admin_conn() |> @endpoint.call(@endpoint.init([]))\n      {:ok, _view, html} = live(sent_conn)\n      doc = Floki.parse_document!(html)\n\n      assert find(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"nav-war-room-dot\"])),\n             \"amendment 4: 'visible from anywhere' — an active session must show the sidebar \" <>\n               \"dot even while viewing the UNRELATED /admin/logs page, not only the dedicated \" <>\n               \"/admin/logs/war-room page\"\n\n      assert find(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"topbar-war-room-pill\"])),\n             \"amendment 4: the topbar pill must likewise be visible from /admin/logs\"\n    end\n  end\nend","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"35e69516-598c-4261-99ae-fb3c92515140","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"7f3562d9-96b3-4d2b-875a-64b7ebd208e4","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"ac24982ebd356bd9e","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5","id":"msg_011CcpifrZFenXT1u6xiAgAU","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"## Accessibility + Design-Consistency Audit — war-room-dedicated-page\n\n**Verdict: 1 violation, 7 advisories**\n\n**Scope checked:** `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/war_room.ex` (new), `server/lib/nebu/admin/war_room_indicator.ex` (new), `server/lib/nebu/admin/layouts.ex`, `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_bubble.ex`, `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live.ex`, `server/priv/static/assets/app.js`, plus `dashboard_live.ex`/`audit_log_live.ex`/`trace_map.ex`/`router.ex` for the indicator wiring. Story read in full including all 7 \"Story Amendments (2026-07-08)\".\n\n**First check (epic-recurring regression — text-text-disabled used for contrast on operable/informative content):** grepped every staged file and every new badge/status/warning/dot/pill for `text-text-disabled` / `text-disabled` — **zero occurrences**. `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.WarRoom` and `WarRoomIndicator`/`layouts.ex` consistently use `text-text-secondary` (#9ca3af, ~7:1 on base-100) and `text-status-error-text` (#fca5a5, 8.51:1 on status-error-bg per the tuned tailwind.config.js token) exactly per the established, previously-fixed pattern. This specific regression class does **not** recur in this diff.\n\n### Violations (blocking)\n\n| # | Requirement | Element | Location | Fix |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | WCAG 1.4.1 Use of Color / 4.1.2 Name, Role, Value — \"the dot on the sidebar nav item ... must not be color-alone (text label + accessible name, not just a colored dot)\" (explicit brief requirement, and the pattern the topbar pill correctly follows) | Sidebar \"War Room\" nav-item recording dot | `server/lib/nebu/admin/layouts.ex:328-333` (`<span :if={Map.get(child, :dot, false)} data-testid=\"nav-war-room-dot\" class=\"tm-recdot inline-block\" aria-hidden=\"true\">`) | The dot is `aria-hidden=\"true\"` and the sibling `{child.label}` text is the static string `\"War Room\"` (from `@logs_nav_children`, `layouts.ex:59`) regardless of `@war_room_active` — a screen-reader user tabbing to this link hears \"War Room\" whether or not a session is recording, with zero indication of state. Contrast with the topbar pill (`layouts.ex:127-138`), which correctly pairs the same `tm-recdot` with visible text (\"War Room recording · mm:ss\"). Add a text-equivalent, e.g. a `sr-only` span (\"(recording)\") alongside the dot, or vary the link's `aria-label`/visible text when `@war_room_active` is true. Confirmed via `server/test/nebu/admin/layouts_war_room_indicator_test.exs`: it only asserts the dot element's *presence*, never any accessible-name/text equivalent — so this gap passed the existing test suite unnoticed. |\n\n### Advisories (non-blocking)\n\n| # | Issue | Location | Recommendation |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 | No explicit focus management on LiveView `navigate` between list ↔ detail (session row click, and the \"← Back to War Room sessions\" link) | `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/war_room.ex:555-562` (back link), `:760-765` (session row `<.link navigate=...>`) | `navigate` triggers a full LiveView remount; nothing moves focus to the new page's `<h1>`. This is systemic across the whole admin app (every sidebar `nav_item` also uses plain `navigate=` with no focus-to-heading hook in `app.js`, confirmed by grep), so it is **not a regression introduced by this story** — flagging because the brief asked explicitly. Worth a follow-up cross-cutting fix (e.g. a small `phx:navigate`-listening hook that focuses `h1` on route change), not blocking this story. |\n| 2 | Topbar recording pill has no `aria-live` region, so an operator working elsewhere in the admin UI when a session starts gets no proactive screen-reader notification (only discoverable by browsing to it) | `server/lib/nebu/admin/layouts.ex:127-138` | Consider `role=\"status\"` (or `aria-live=\"polite\"`) on `#topbar-war-room-pill`, matching the `role=\"status\"` pattern already used for the copy-to-markdown toast (`war_room.ex:818`). |\n| 3 | Copy-to-markdown toast (`role=\"status\"`) will not re-announce to screen readers on a second/third click, since `@copy_toast` is set to the identical string each time and LiveView sends no DOM patch for an unchanged assign | `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/war_room.ex:248-249`, `:815-822` | Reset `@copy_toast` to `\"\"` briefly (or append a nonce/timestamp) so repeated clicks re-trigger the live-region announcement. Not blocking — first-use case works correctly and this mirrors a common, low-severity toast pitfall. |\n| 4 | `:readonly` `TraceMapBubble` still renders `aria-pressed=\"false\"` on a `disabled`, non-interactive `<button>` — a toggle-state ARIA attribute on an element that is explicitly documented as \"never a filter\" | `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_bubble.ex:91,94-95` | Omit `aria-pressed` entirely when `mode == :readonly` (it's a legend, not a toggle) to avoid a misleading semantic signal. |\n| 5 | AC2 requires \"Session ID (UUID, truncated or full — operator's choice)\" as a **displayed** row field; the current list row only carries it as `data-room-id` (not visible/announced text) | `server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/war_room.ex:762-780` | Add a visible/announced session-ID fragment (e.g. a truncated `<span>{String.slice(session.id, 0, 8)}</span>`) to satisfy AC2's literal text, though `reason` + `time_range` + `started_by` already give reasonable row identification. |\n| 6 | `opacity-80` applied to `text-status-error-text` inside the topbar pill (for the `started_by` suffix) erodes the codebase's carefully-tuned 8.51:1 contrast ratio down to roughly ~5.9:1 against `status-error-bg` (still WCAG AA-compliant, but this project explicitly hand-tunes and documents contrast ratios in `tailwind.config.js` — ad hoc opacity modifiers bypass that rigor) | `server/lib/nebu/admin/layouts.ex:137` | Low priority since it still passes AA; consider a dedicated, pre-computed muted token instead of `opacity-*` if this pattern is used elsewhere too. |\n| 7 | The `pill--warroom` class applied to the topbar pill (`layouts.ex:133`) has no matching CSS rule anywhere in `server/assets/css/app.css` or `server/priv/static/assets/app.css` — it's an inert marker name left over from mirroring the approved dummy's own `.pill--warroom` class, with all actual styling coming from the adjacent Tailwind utility classes | `server/lib/nebu/admin/layouts.ex:133` | Cosmetic only (no functional/a11y impact) — either wire real CSS to it or drop the now-meaningless class name to avoid confusing future maintainers. |\n\n### Design fidelity\n\nReuse of `.tm-recdot` (from `trace-map-leitstand`) for both the sidebar dot and topbar pill, and the `status-error-bg`/`status-error-text` token pairing, are consistent with the Obsidian theme and the approved `trace-map-leitstand-dummy.html` reference (`.pill--warroom`/`.recdot`). Warning banners (`alert alert-warning`/`alert alert-error`) and form styling (`form-control`/`input-bordered`/`select-bordered`) match the established DaisyUI conventions used elsewhere in this epic (retention settings, node-controls inspector). No new component patterns were invented outside the established library.\n\n### CSP / keyboard / escaping — no findings\n\n- CSP `script-src 'self'`: `WarRoomPill` hook (`server/priv/static/assets/app.js`, `mounted`/`updated`/`destroyed`/`tick`) and copy-to-clipboard (`document.addEventListener(\"nebu:copy-thread\", ...)`, reused unmodified) both use only external-file listeners; no inline JS or `onclick` attributes anywhere in the diff.\n- Keyboard operability: session rows are genuine `<.link navigate=...>` (`<a>`) elements with a visible `focus-visible:ring-2` style; the scope `<select name=\"scope\">` and exclusive-scope `<input type=\"checkbox\">`/label-wrapped inputs are native controls, confirmed reachable via normal Tab order and activatable with Enter/Space — genuinely supports the story's own keyboard E2E scenario, not just passing it by accident (the round-2 bug fixes were a missing `phx-connected` wait in the E2E step, not a markup gap).\n- HTML-escaping: `reason`, `scope_label`, `filter_summary`, `exclusive_scope`/`exclusive_other`, and captured-log `message`/`route_template` fields are rendered exclusively through ordinary HEEx `{...}` interpolation (including inside the `data-copy-text` attribute for markdown export) — no `raw/1`, `Phoenix.HTML.raw/1`, or `safe_html` helper touches any of them anywhere in `war_room.ex`.\n- 404 detail page (`war_room.ex:841-845`) correctly still shows the \"← Back to War Room sessions\" link (rendered unconditionally for `@mode == :detail` at `war_room.ex:555-562`, independent of whether `@session` is nil), satisfying AC6.\n\n**Live-stack note:** `docker compose ps` shows the stack running; `curl http://localhost:8008/admin/logs/war-room` returns a 302 (auth redirect), confirming the route resolves, but verifying it reflects these exact staged (uncommitted/undeployed) changes was not reliable without a `make redeploy` cycle and an admin login flow, so this audit relied on static code analysis per the audit method's documented fallback."}],"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":233,"cache_read_input_tokens":118376,"output_tokens":3625,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":233},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":3625,"cache_read_input_tokens":118376,"cache_creation_input_tokens":233,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":233,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CcpifqSHQsR6NPXCqa2Ny","attributionAgent":"nebu-ux-reviewer","type":"assistant","uuid":"0c0c3587-d2d3-48ad-a342-88ab47509555","timestamp":"2026-07-08T14:07:02.609Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}
